That is one reason plain SFT is a weak baseline here. It can memorize seen prompt patterns, but it does not naturally optimize for robustness to new forms of pressure or strategic settings.

The key mechanism is targeted adversarial training in embedding space. Instead of searching over discrete prompt edits, which is too expensive, the method perturbs token embeddings within an epsilon-bounded region and uses projected signed gradient ascent for K steps to approximately maximize log p_theta(y- | x + delta). This yields a local worst-case prompt perturbation that makes the dishonest continuation more likely. The outer optimization then trains the model to suppress y- and favor y+ under those adversarially perturbed inputs. In effect, the model is not just trained to answer honestly on the original prompt, but to remain honest in a neighborhood of semantically nearby adversarial variants.

The paper also pays attention to capability preservation. CATCH is trained with a utility dataset in addition to honesty targets, and the authors explicitly study how the choice of that utility data affects general performance retention. That is a meaningful detail: if the method only improved honesty by broadly dampening compliance or increasing refusals, it would be much less interesting. Compared with inference-time honesty steering, CATCH aims for a more stable trained behavior. Compared with large-scale honesty SFT, it relies on adversarial objectives to get more transfer from much less data.4f:T67d,Several concrete points are clear from the full text provided. The training set is small, around 200 targets built from self-reporting scenarios. The method is tested on two instruction-tuned models: Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and Qwen3.6-27B. Generalization is evaluated across three distinct dishonesty settings: instructed dishonesty on MASK, incentive-driven dishonesty in Among Us, and training-induced concealment on AuditBench. That benchmark spread matters because it tests transfer across different causal sources of dishonesty rather than within one prompt format.

The headline empirical claim is that CATCH outperforms all baselines and is competitive with the strongest honesty steering method on MASK, while surpassing an honesty-SFT implementation trained on roughly 50 times more honesty data. That is the main result to pay attention to: a small targeted dataset plus adversarial training may produce more transferable honesty behavior than much larger direct supervision.

Another important result concerns the utility dataset. The paper reports that utility-data choice strongly affects capability preservation and proposes a better selection strategy that preserves general ability better than prior honesty steering. The excerpt does not include the tables, so I cannot reliably provide exact benchmark scores or deltas. When reading the experiments, the main checks should be: whether honesty gains come with increased refusal behavior, which capability benchmarks are used for retention, how sensitive results are to epsilon, attack steps, and toward/away loss weighting, and whether the gains are overly tied to self-reporting-style prompts.50:T5e9,No new architecture is proposed; instead the paper builds an analysis model. Data is assumed to arrive in groups (tasks), each task being generated by a sparse set of latent factors. Tasks share factors, and task similarity is modeled via latent-factor overlap (illustrated as a bipartite task–factor graph). Under this view, inference-time learning is modeled as: shared components are carried by internal representations of inputs/outputs, while the task-specific mapping between these representations must be learned at inference time by an inner learner.
From this setup, the paper argues for:
- Rich representation (“gather broadly”): if representations only capture what is minimally sufficient for iid generalization, many new tasks become unreachable; open-world adaptation needs a broader feature basis that supports recombination of latent factors.
- Predictive disentanglement (“group predictively”): beyond richness, representations should be partitioned into matched low-dimensional groups across inputs and outputs, and prediction should occur within aligned groups. This reduces the effective degrees of freedom the inner learner must fit for a new task, improving sample efficiency.
It further predicts: (1) inference-time learning requires a consistent inner learner; otherwise adding more related-task examples in context can degrade performance; (2) longer contexts can help only if the target task and related tasks are handled separately to avoid harmful coupling/negative transfer.51:T402,The key method is parameter-trajectory crosscoding. Instead of fitting sparse dictionaries to activations, the method fits one sparse dictionary to the readout rows of all tokens across all checkpoints. Each token has a shared sparse code, while each feature has checkpoint-specific decoder directions and gates. This keeps feature identities aligned over training and avoids the unstable matching problem that would arise from training a separate sparse autoencoder at every checkpoint. Rows are centered and scaled per checkpoint, so feature norms track structural reorganization rather than only global norm growth. The second diagnostic is readout swapping: hold hidden states fixed and decode them with readouts from other checkpoints. Together with linear probes, this separates hidden-state availability from native readout expression. The logit is treated as a dot product between hidden state and readout row, so changes in logits can be attributed to changes in the representation, the readout, or their interaction.52:T428,This matters for pretraining research in several ways. First, it shows that having the full context in the prompt does not remove working-memory-like bottlenecks; the bottleneck can move from storage to readout. Second, it narrows the scope of a common explanation—'attention is not sharp enough'—to a much smaller regime. That explanation appears valid for very small single-layer transformers, but not for modern multilayer pretrained LLMs, where content interference dominates. Third, it has direct implications for long-context modeling and retrieval-style architectures. If failures come mainly from readout contamination rather than target localization, then sharper attention, better positional schemes, or more exact retrieval may not be the main fix. The more relevant design question becomes how representations preserve item identity across layers and how downstream computation avoids mixing competing content. The contribution is not a new model class; it is a careful mechanistic disambiguation with cross-family, cross-scale, and causal support.53:T809,The experimental design is unusually clean. The authors present N-back as an in-context task to 15 pretrained LLMs, using the log-probability difference between 'm' and '-' as the decision variable and calibrating a fixed threshold. They report d-prime rather than raw accuracy, which is the right choice here because it separates sensitivity from threshold effects.

The key contribution is the mechanism test. First, they identify the attention heads that actually carry the task instead of averaging over all heads. They then inspect where those heads attend on lure-error trials. Under the precision account, attention should systematically miss the target on error trials; under the interference account, attention should still land on the target most of the time.

Second, they test decodability from internal activations. Even when the model outputs the wrong answer, can a simple classifier still recover whether the repeated letter occurred at the target position versus a lure position? If yes, the relevant information is present in the representation and the failure lies downstream in readout rather than retrieval.

Third, they run causal interventions. One intervention sharpens attention. The other suppresses or erases the content of competing items. If sharpening helps little while removing competitors helps a lot, the bottleneck is not target localization but content interference. That is the pattern they report.

Fourth, they use small transformers trained from scratch to establish boundary conditions. A single-layer transformer behaves much more like the precision account predicts, because one softmax has to do both localization and content aggregation. Adding a second layer changes the picture: the model can first localize coarsely and then reorganize or clean up the representation, so precision is no longer the dominant limitation. This controlled comparison explains why earlier analyses on tiny models could attribute N-back errors to softmax precision while that explanation does not transfer to modern pretrained LLMs.54:T654,The study covers 15 pretrained LLMs from three families, spanning 70M to 14B parameters, plus minimal transformers trained from scratch. Each sequence has length 24, letters are drawn from 20 consonants, each test sequence contains four lures, thresholds are calibrated on 200 sequences, and evaluation uses 1,000 held-out sequences per model and per N. The paper first reproduces the expected behavioral pattern: d-prime declines as N increases, and errors cluster around nearby lures such as N±1.

The more important findings are mechanistic. On the heads that actually support the task, attention on error trials still tends to peak at the target rather than shifting systematically to the lure, which weakens the precision account. A simple classifier can decode target-versus-lure information from internal activations even on trials where the model ultimately makes the lure error, showing that the needed information survives inside the network. In causal tests, removing competing item content improves performance much more than merely sharpening attention, which supports a readout bottleneck. The scale analysis shows that this conclusion is stable across roughly a 200x parameter range; larger models distribute the computation across more heads, but the bottleneck does not move. The small-model comparison shows that the precision account does hold in a single-layer transformer, while a second layer is enough to substantially relax that limitation. The value of the experiments is less about one headline number and more about the consistency of these patterns across families, sizes, and interventions.55:T493,For a quick pass, focus on three parts. First, read the introduction carefully where the two accounts are formalized and where they make opposite predictions about attention on error trials and intervention outcomes. Second, inspect the figures on task-carrying heads and on decodability of target versus lure information from internal activations; these are central to the argument. Third, read the causal intervention section comparing attention sharpening with competitor-content erasure. That comparison carries most of the paper's weight.

If you have 20–30 minutes, read the introduction, the main mechanism figures, Section 4, and Section 5, then skim Section 6 on the single-layer versus two-layer transformer contrast. If you want to go deeper, examine how task-carrying heads are defined and whether that selection could bias the conclusion. Also ask what exactly 'interference' means computationally here: value mixing inside attention, accumulation in the residual stream, or a later linear readout failure. That question connects directly to long-context failures in modern LLMs, especially cases where retrieval succeeds but answer formation still breaks.56:T497,The contribution is an experimental design rather than a new model.
- Setup: N=10 agents (same base model) discuss one factual QA item for T=16 rounds. Each round every agent speaks once; speaking order is reshuffled each round. Agents condition only on the last H=5 rounds to control context.
- Adversary: k∈{0,1,3} attacker-controlled agents repeatedly advocate a fixed, plausible false answer. The attacker only posts messages (context-only attack).
- Dual measurement: after the initial state and after every round, each benign agent is privately probed in a separate call (not visible to peers) for its own answer. In parallel, each public chat message is labeled for stance toward the false claim (endorse/reject/hedge/ignore).
- Infection labeling: private answers are judged as true/manipulated/mixed/uncertain; only “manipulated” counts as infection, avoiding conflating uncertainty with belief change.
- The key analytical lens is the mismatch structure between public stance and private belief, revealing that the dominant failure is often private shift without public endorsement (minority-influence-like), which makes transcript-only monitoring unreliable.57:T433,Evaluated across 6 models and 3 factual-QA datasets (18 model–dataset cells) under k=0/1/3 attackers.
Main quantitative findings reported in the paper:
- No attacker: final infection is only 0.6% of benign agents.
- One attacker: final infection rises to 44.8%.
- Three attackers: final infection reaches 60.5%.
- Hidden infection gap (k=3): across exposed turns, private false belief occurs 51.6% of the time, while public endorsement of the false claim is only 19.7%.
- Mismatch asymmetry: when agents privately remain correct, they almost never endorse the false claim publicly (2.3% of turns). When agents privately become incorrect, they still often hedge or ignore publicly (58% of turns).
- Weak correction: agents who privately keep the true answer explicitly reject the false claim in only 61.9% of their public turns.
- Knowledge is not robustness: two models with similar factual knowledge (~85%) can differ drastically in how many facts they abandon under attack (25% vs 80%), suggesting conversational susceptibility is not explained by static knowledge alone.58:T419,This is not a pretraining recipe paper, but it is useful for pretraining researchers because it improves the methodology for studying internal representations. A lot of current SAE analysis still relies on one-off feature stories: feature A went down, feature B went up, therefore the model changed its internal reasoning. Those stories are often fragile under prompt reformulation. RSS is an attempt to replace anecdotal feature tracking with a reproducible support selected across prompt variants and validated on held-out variants. That makes it more relevant for interpretability, alignment audits, and robustness analysis. For foundation-model work, the main value is methodological: if you want to study how tokenization choices, stylistic shifts, instruction templates, or synthetic data formats alter internal representations, you need a way to distinguish stable redistribution from incidental perturbation. The paper also makes an important conceptual separation: recurrent feature change is not the same thing as downstream causal effect.59:T975,The central object is the Redistribution Sensitivity Score, or RSS. It does not simply rank features by absolute change. Instead, it requires three properties at once: large response magnitude, recurrence across multiple transformed variants, and directional consistency. For a clean prompt and a set of transformed variants, the authors compute the activation change of each SAE feature at the aligned target position. They first form a candidate pool using average absolute change h_f so that opposite signs do not cancel. Then they measure how often each feature responds above a threshold tau and whether those responses mostly share the same sign, producing a recurrence score r_f and a consistency score c_f. Only features satisfying r_f >= rho and c_f >= kappa are eligible. The final support S is the top-K eligible features by response magnitude, and MeanRSS summarizes the average response strength of that support.

Two design choices matter. First, selection and evaluation are separated. The support and its dominant directions are chosen on selector variants and then frozen; evaluation is performed on disjoint held-out variants without recomputing the support. That is the right way to avoid post hoc feature storytelling. Second, RSS is defined without using readout weights. It first answers which representation changes are stable, and only afterward asks whether those changes matter for a downstream linear readout.

The readout analysis is also well structured. For a fixed linear probe, the paper gives an exact decomposition of each recurrent feature change into its contribution to the readout logit. That contribution depends on activation magnitude, probe weight, directional alignment, and the clean margin to the decision threshold. This separates three levels that are often conflated: representation redistribution, readout score change, and threshold crossing. A feature can change reliably and still have little behavioral effect if its readout weight is small or if multiple contributions cancel.

The paper also provides a finite-sample directional stability guarantee. With n=5 and rho=kappa=0.8, every selected feature must have at least four responses in the dominant direction and at most one in the opposite direction, so removing one observation cannot flip the selected sign. That makes the selected support a more reproducible object than a one-shot ranking of changed features.5a:T6a8,The experiments cover 27 pretrained SAEs spanning eight language models. The empirical questions are whether prompt transformations induce redistribution profiles that reproduce on held-out variants, and whether those recurrent changes explain variation in frozen safety readouts. The main findings are threefold.

First, the supports selected by RSS produce reproducible redistribution profiles on held-out variants, which suggests that RSS is capturing stable structure rather than one-off feature fluctuations on the selector set. Second, on two frozen safety-readout audit tasks, the supports are selected without any readout information, yet they still reconstruct a substantial fraction of held-out logit change. That indicates a stable link between recurrent representation change and downstream readout variation. Third, when RSS supports are combined with the clean margin, they retain strong utility for predicting recurrent failure. The reported numbers are: median support sizes of 20 and 19 features for the two tasks, with recurrent-failure AUROCs of 0.935 and 0.958, compared with 0.965 and 0.964 using full erosion. The gap is modest, which means a very small recurrent support preserves most of the audit signal.

The most useful takeaway is not the AUROC itself but the decomposition. The experiments show that recurrent representation change does not map one-to-one to failure. Some recurrent changes alter the readout score without crossing the threshold; others only become predictive once the clean margin is taken into account. That moves SAE analysis from 'which features changed' to 'how changed features interact with a readout to produce or fail to produce a behavioral error.'5b:T456,This is a skim paper, but the method deserves careful attention. Start with the introduction and method to understand why the authors separate redistribution, readout contribution, and decision failure. Then focus on the RSS definition itself: candidate pool construction, recurrence, directional consistency, support selection, and held-out evaluation. After that, read the experiments on the two frozen safety readouts, especially the support sizes, held-out logit reconstruction, and failure AUROC when combined with the clean margin.

If you only have about 20 minutes, read the method section, Figure 1, and the main results tables on held-out reproducibility and AUROC. If you want to go deeper, inspect the supplementary details on probe scaling, operator-level averaging, and the full-erosion baseline. Those choices determine whether RSS is isolating genuinely stable mechanism-related change or just a byproduct of preprocessing. The right framing while reading is that the paper is not claiming SAE features are causal atoms; it is proposing a stricter reproducibility filter for SAE-based analysis.5c:Ta7b,核心做法是把“特征组合的下游行为”形式化成一个 set function，然后用类似 ANOVA/高阶交互分解的方式度量剩余交互能量。

1) 子集干预（subset intervention）定义
- 对每个输入 x，在某层某 token 位置取激活 h_x。
- SAE 给出 top-K 活跃特征集合 N_x，以及每个特征的解码贡献 δ_i(x)=z_i(x)d_i（文中用 D_x 作为 K 个贡献列向量的矩阵）。
- 构造一个“固定底座”激活 h_x^0 = h_x - Σ_{i∈N_x} δ_i(x)，它包含未选特征贡献 + 重构残差等所有固定内容。
- 对任意子集 S⊆N_x，构造干预激活 ~h_x(S)=h_x^0 + Σ_{i∈S} δ_i(x)。这样 S=N_x 时恢复原激活，S=∅ 时只保留底座。
- 选一个标量行为读出 ψ（例如某类输出概率、某个评分器输出、某个行为指标），定义 set function g_x(S)=ψ(F(~h_x(S)))。

2) 低阶可加近似与 SAE Additivity Residuals（SAR）
- 用“至多 r 阶交互”的函数类 𝒜_r(N) 去近似 g_x(S)：r=1 只允许单特征效应相加；r=2 允许加入 pairwise 项；更高阶同理。
- 在子集分布 μ 下做 L2 投影 Π_r g_x，定义 SAR_r 为归一化后的剩余能量：直观上就是“用 r 阶模型解释不了的行为方差占比”。
- 进一步定义 Δ_2 = SAR_1 - SAR_2 表示加入 pairwise 后解释回来的结构量，ρ_2=Δ_2/SAR_1 表示 pairwise 在一阶残差中占的比例。

3) 为什么强调“独立子集采样”
- 文中在“对每个特征独立采样是否保留”的 μ 下，SAR 有精确的交互分解：SAR_r 等于 r 阶以上交互项的归一化能量（而不是一个仅凭经验的指标）。这让 SAR 具有可解释的统计含义：你测到的残差就是高阶交互的能量。

4) 坐标选择 vs 子空间：sum-preserving random rotations
- 为了区分“是坐标本身更可组合”还是“同一子空间下都一样”，作者构造正交矩阵 Q，要求 Q·1=1（保持总和不变），把 K 个贡献在子空间内做随机旋转：D'_x = D_x Q。
- 这样旋转后 span(D'_x)=span(D_x) 且 Σ列向量不变，保证“选中子空间”和“总贡献”一致，只改变坐标系。
- 对 learned SAE 坐标与旋转坐标分别计算 SAR_1、SAR_2 等，比较可组合性差异。

5) 与实际使用对齐：coalition ordering 与 steering
- 论文不仅看拟合误差，还看“对不同 feature 子集的效果排序是否保持”（coalition ordering error）。他们给出 SAR 与排序误差之间的界。
- 在组合 steering 实验里，把多个 SAE 方向组合去改变行为，比较一阶/二阶模型对“哪个组合更有效”的排序保真度（rank fidelity）。5d:T90a,The method turns “feature-combination effects” into a set-function and measures residual interaction energy via low-order projections.

1) Subset intervention as a set-function
- For input x, take an activation h_x at a fixed layer/token.
- Select top-K active SAE features N_x with decoded contributions δ_i(x)=z_i(x)d_i.
- Define a fixed remainder h_x^0 = h_x - Σ_{i∈N_x} δ_i(x), which keeps everything else constant (unselected content + reconstruction residual).
- For any subset S⊆N_x, intervene with ~h_x(S)=h_x^0 + Σ_{i∈S} δ_i(x).
- For a scalar behavioral readout ψ, define g_x(S)=ψ(F(~h_x(S))). This isolates behavioral changes attributable to the retained selected contributions.

2) SAE Additivity Residuals (SAR)
- Approximate g_x using function classes that include interactions up to order r: r=1 (additive main effects), r=2 (additive + pairwise terms), etc.
- Under a subset distribution μ, take the L2(μ) projection Π_r g_x and define SAR_r as the normalized residual energy (variance not explained by order ≤r).
- Define Δ_2 = SAR_1 − SAR_2 and ρ_2 = Δ_2 / SAR_1 to quantify how much of the first-order residual is recovered by pairwise structure.

3) Why independent subset sampling matters
- With independent inclusion of each feature (a product measure μ), SAR admits an exact interaction decomposition: SAR_r equals the normalized energy of interactions above order r. This gives SAR a clean statistical meaning rather than a heuristic score.

4) Coordinate choice vs subspace: sum-preserving random rotations
- Construct orthogonal Q with Q·1=1 and rotate the K contribution vectors: D'_x = D_x Q.
- This preserves the selected subspace span(D_x) and the total contribution (sum of columns), keeping the downstream network and fixed remainder unchanged.
- Compare SAR and ordering metrics between learned SAE coordinates and these matched rotations to isolate the effect of coordinate system.

5) Practical endpoint: coalition ordering and steering
- Beyond fit error, the paper evaluates how well low-order models preserve the ranking over feature subsets (coalition ordering), and provides bounds relating SAR to ordering error.
- In a combined steering setting, it measures rank fidelity of predicted vs actual steering effectiveness for feature combinations.5e:T434,Key empirical claims reported in the paper:
- Across 3 model/SAE pipelines and 6 behavioral readouts (with extensions to more pipelines and depths), learned SAE coordinates consistently show lower first-order residuals than sum-preserving random rotations, indicating coordinate systems can be more compositional than arbitrary bases of the same subspace.
- Adding pairwise terms improves held-out coalition ordering in all 18 tested settings and beats parameter-matched random third-order controls, suggesting the gains are specifically due to meaningful pairwise structure.
- In a combined SAE steering experiment, pairwise modeling increases rank fidelity from 0.5874 to 0.7790.
- In a matched comparison of 6 SAEs, SAR and reconstruction quality do not always track each other, implying “good reconstruction” is not sufficient as a proxy for “good compositionality”.

(The provided excerpt does not include all tables/appendix numbers such as exact model names, K, or per-readout absolute metrics; those would need to be pulled from the full experimental section.)5f:T616,The method has a data side and a training side. The data side is called Refine. Starting from simple multimodal questions and successful candidate reasoning chains, it extracts an entity chain and then applies three controllable operators. Expand appends L evidence-supported hops after the original chain, turning the original answer into an intermediate node and forcing the agent to continue searching. Blur selects M internal nodes and attaches W retrievable but indirect facts to each, replacing explicit entity mentions with searchable clues and increasing disambiguation difficulty. Visualize selects N nodes near the end of the chain, retains up to P retrieved images per node, and injects visual evidence requirements so that solving the task requires both textual and visual retrieval. The pipeline also includes validation and filtering to reduce shortcuts from parametric knowledge or alternative evidence paths. On the training side, the paper uses joint RL and OPD. RL uses answer rewards plus lightweight behavior penalties to discourage redundant verification and premature stopping. OPD is not applied as full-trajectory hard distillation; instead, token-level teacher guidance is applied on the student’s own imperfect rollouts, with strength modulated by a shaped reward gap, and the OPD coefficient is exponentially annealed over training. The motivation is clear: pure RL gives only a coarse scalar signal, while full distillation can overwrite exploration with teacher behavior. The proposed scheme tries to keep the benefits of both.60:T405,The main evaluation uses synthetic financial transaction QA. The inner loop roughly triples coverage across 13 starting operator sets. The outer loop extends three disjoint starting sets beyond their original closures, with a no-operator control isolating the effect of newly induced operators. The grown DSL matches an independent human-authored gold extractor on every compute type. The admission test is the most informative result: under an independent reference, it accepts none of 138 buggy operator variants, including variants designed to pass simple agreement checks. Baselines such as admit-if-it-runs, self-consistency, and compression admit most of them. The gate remains sound when the reference comes from a separate call and from a different model family, Gemma-3-27B. The same loop, with only a schema-adapting parser changed, also self-extends on business-ledger and clinical-encounter QA. Mistral-Small-24B and Llama-3.3-70B show comparable self-extension, with yield tracking each model’s fluency in the DSL.61:T7a1,FedSkillGit is explicitly inspired by Git-style incremental collaboration. The server maintains a shared skill document d and a global experience document e. Each client has private train/validation splits and four LLM-based roles: an executor for task execution, a skill optimizer for local refinement, a sanitizer for privacy cleaning, and an auditor for private-value checking. Instead of uploading a rewritten full skill document, the client generates semantic commits: localized skill patches together with supporting experience summaries.

The pipeline has several stages. First, local skill evolution: the client executes tasks using the current global skill and global experience context, collects local successes and failures, and asks the optimizer to produce revisions. Second, two-stage privacy filtering: a sanitizer rewrites or removes sensitive content, and an auditor checks whether the commit still contains private values or environment-specific details. This is not formal differential privacy; it is semantic privacy filtering performed by LLM agents. Third, cross-round experience reuse: the server maintains a global experience document that accumulates reusable evidence over rounds and redistributes it to clients, reducing redundant rediscovery and making local optimization more informed. Fourth, experience-aware aggregation: the server uses a merge agent to combine commits with reference to the global experience document, rather than simply concatenating or voting over full documents. Fifth, version validation: a candidate updated skill is only published if it passes validation.

Relative to straightforward federated baselines, the key differences are the shift from document-level to commit-level updates, the inclusion of evidence alongside edits, and the explicit use of a shared experience memory. That makes the system closer to collaborative knowledge editing with validation than to standard federated averaging.62:T63a,The paper reports experiments on three datasets and three LM families. The headline result is that FedSkillGit outperforms the strongest baseline by up to 22% and approaches centralized performance. From the provided full-text excerpt, the most reliable takeaways are as follows.

First, overall effectiveness: the gains appear across multiple datasets and backbones, suggesting that the improvement is not tied to a single model family or task distribution. The paper positions the method against federated baselines that evolve and aggregate full skill documents, and the reported gap indicates that the update representation itself matters.

Second, communication and privacy motivation: because the method transmits semantic commits rather than full documents, the communication cost should scale with newly acquired knowledge instead of accumulated skill length. When reading the full paper, the most important tables and plots to verify are communication curves, average commit size, and how document growth behaves over rounds.

Third, ablations: the architecture strongly suggests useful ablations around removing privacy filtering, removing the global experience document, disabling version validation, and replacing commit-level aggregation with full-document aggregation. Those ablations are likely where the paper justifies why each component exists.

The excerpt provided here does not include the full experimental tables, exact model sizes, or dataset sizes, so precise numbers beyond the reported “up to 22%” should be checked directly in the original paper before citation.63:T46d,LEASH formulates weak-to-strong escalation as a budget-constrained sequential stopping problem, where the weak model’s estimated failure risk acts as a proxy for the benefit of escalation. It builds a representation bank from a small set of held-out tasks by recording hidden states on prompt and action tokens and labeling them with task-level and subgoal-level outcomes. At test time, the current prompt or execution trace is scored by a Mahalanobis-distance criterion: the hidden state is uncertain if it lies closer to past failures than to past successes. This score drives two mechanisms. First, an initial gate decides whether to escalate at the start, while a runtime gate monitors the weak agent during execution. Second, after handoff, the weak model becomes a shadow observer for the strong model, checking the trajectory every three steps. If uncertainty is high, the pending action is rolled back and re-decided once with higher reasoning effort. The main difference from prior escalation schemes is that no additional monitor training, external knowledge base, reference model, or per-step multi-sampling is required.64:T80b,The framework is a sequential-auction MDP. A task is modeled as a finite-horizon MDP, but actions are not chosen by one global policy. Instead, a society of agents bids for the right to act on the current state. The winning agent transforms the state and then resells the resulting state into the next auction. When the task reaches a terminal solution, the final reward is propagated backward through the resale prices, implementing a bucket-brigade style temporal credit assignment.

The starting point is the theory of Chang et al., where Vickrey auctions align local bidding incentives with the globally optimal MDP policy under perfect competition. The paper argues that this assumption is economically unrealistic for LLM societies. With exact clones, the winner pays essentially its true valuation, so expected profit goes to zero and participation becomes irrational. At the other extreme, if an agent is the only eligible buyer at some step, it behaves as a monopsonist and bids the minimum needed to win. The clearing price collapses, upstream contributors are underpaid, and the multi-step chain becomes unsustainable.

The proposed shift is to study imperfect competition. Agents are similar enough to compete, but differentiated by inference cost, prompting, heuristics, and capability. Since LLMs are poor zero-shot estimators of their own success probabilities and token costs, the paper does not rely on direct self-evaluation. Instead, each agent learns a lightweight valuation policy with reinforcement learning to decide whether to bid and how much to shade its bid. The paper benchmarks four bidding strategies: Mirror Descent, Thompson Sampling, Bayes-Optimal, and PPO. It also compares multiple auction rules, including first-price, second-price, and a credit-conserving Vickrey variant.

Methodologically, the contribution is less about one new algorithm and more about building an experimental framework that combines sequential auctions, LLM agents, learned valuation policies, and market-density analysis under imperfect competition.65:T4ed,The experiments have two roles. One is to validate the economic failure mode: in synthetic settings, the authors show monopsony failure, where lack of competition collapses bids and prevents value from flowing upstream. The other is to test whether realistic intermediate regimes work: they run market-density sweeps and compare decentralized markets against centralized orchestration baselines on task success and agent profit.

The domains mentioned in the paper include math reasoning and a newly constructed synthetic news market. The main empirical conclusion is that no competition fails, but perfect clones are not required either. A middle regime of imperfect competition is sufficient to sustain positive profits while also achieving higher task success than centralized orchestration baselines. That is the main result to retain.

The provided full-text excerpt does not expose all numeric tables, so it would be unsafe to invent exact gains. What is clear is the experimental structure: systematic comparison across auction types, density sweeps over market competition, multiple bidding learners, and evaluation on both accuracy and profitability. That last point matters, because accuracy alone would miss whether the market is economically viable.15:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T01:05:30Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21061.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文研究离散扩散模型在零样本条件生成中的 score mismatch：训练时学到的是无条件分布的 score，推理时却要从条件分布或集合约束分布采样，这会使反向 CTMC 的 rate matrix 偏离真实条件反向过程。","importance":"这篇论文对 diffusion LM / discrete diffusion 的预训练范式有理论价值。它没有提出新的大规模文本模型，而是解释了一个实际问题：为什么把无条件离散扩散模型直接拿来做 infilling、completion、约束生成时，硬性 clamping 条件并不总是等价于真实条件采样。对基座研究员的启发在于，离散扩散的条件能力不能只看采样技巧，还要看 forward noising rate 与条件 score 的兼容性。尤其是 absorbing rate 和 uniform rate 的差异，会直接影响零样本条件采样是否引入额外偏差。","technical_approach":"核心技术是把 score mismatch 写进离散扩散反向过程的 rate matrix，并给出非渐近 KL 上界。连续扩散里常把 mismatch 理解为反向 Gaussian 均值扰动；离散扩散不同，状态转移由 CTMC rate matrix 控制，score 错误会改变离散状态之间的反向跳转率。论文将采样误差分成两类：一类是没有 mismatch 时已有的离散扩散采样误差，另一类是由训练分布 score 与目标分布 score 不一致带来的 bias。这个 bias 不是简单的 L2 score error，而与 score entropy 对应的 Bregman divergence 有关。\n\n在 Conditional SEDD 上，论文分析 hard clamping 的真实含义。hard clamping 是每一步把观测坐标强行设回给定值，但这不一定等价于条件反向过程。对 absorbing rate，如果固定观测是非 MASK token，未观测坐标上的 hard-clamp score 与真实条件 score 一致，因此上界里不需要额外 score-mismatch bias。对 uniform rate，这个等价性一般不成立，hard clamping 会改变反向转移结构并引入偏差。\n\n论文还把固定观测推广到 coordinate-wise set constraints。集合约束下，每个受约束位置允许多个候选 token。作者用 assignment mixture 表示目标分布：先在可行 assignment 上分配概率，再对每个 assignment 复用固定观测采样器。这样可以把固定观测的收敛保证提升到集合约束场景，并显式控制 assignment 分布近似带来的额外误差。","experiments":"主要贡献是理论分析。关键结果是 KL divergence 上界：目标分布与采样分布的差距由基础采样误差和 score-mismatch bias 共同决定。对 Conditional SEDD，论文给出 rate-dependent 的偏差刻画：absorbing rate 在固定非 MASK 观测下更接近真实条件反向过程；uniform rate 下 hard clamping 通常会产生额外 mismatch。集合约束部分给出 mixture 形式的误差分解，将每个 assignment 的固定观测误差与 assignment 权重近似误差分开。全文材料中没有看到大规模语言模型或文本生成 benchmark clamping 分析，注意 absorbing rate 与 uniform rate 的结论差异；第三，看 set constraints 的 assignment mixture 构造，这是把固定观测推广到词表候选集合、受限改写、lexical simplification 等任务的关键。若只关心 diffusion LM 预训练，可重点关注 noising rate 选择对后续条件能力的影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies score mismatch in zero-shot conditional sampling for discrete diffusion models: the model is trained to score an At ultra-long context it stops paying, because a drafter that attends over the full prefix inherits the target's context-dependent cost, so the speedup it was meant to provide first erodes and then reverses. The standard response is to build a long-context drafter, by training or fine-tuning one, or by using a multi-token-prediction head shipped inside the target checkpoint. We argue this is unnecessary, be","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-14T19:54:59Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_20865.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在超长上下文（如百万 token）下，推测解码（Speculative Decoding）的加速效果会严重衰减甚至变为负收益，因为草稿模型（drafter）的注意力计算成本随上下文长度线性增长，导致其不再是一个“廉价”的近似模型。","importance":"打破了“长上下文推测解码必须训练长上下文草稿模型”的固有认知。提出了一种无需微调、无需修改权重的即插即用方案，使得现有的短上下文草稿模型能够直接在超长上下文下保持高效加速，极大地降低了长文本推理优化的门槛。","technical_approach":"核心洞察是：现有的草稿模型（如 EAGLE 系列）是“目标条件化”的，其输入已经包含了目标模型在整个前缀上计算的隐藏状态，因此远距离上下文信息已经通过这些特征传递给草稿模型，无需其自身进行全局注意力计算。基于此，作者提出两种免训练的干预手段：1) Windowing（窗口化）：限制草稿模型的注意力范围为最近的 W 个 token，使其计算成本与总上下文长度 N 无关；2) Index Re-basing（索引重基）：将草稿模型的旋转位置编码（RoPE）索引映射回其训练时的有限范围 [0, Pmax) 内（通过模运算），避免索引越界导致的静默失效。两者结合，在 vLLM 中实现为纯推理期的 patch。","experiments":"在 2M token 上下文下，使用冻结的短上下文草稿模型，结合窗口化和索引重基后，保留了 8k 上下文下 90% 和 119% 的加速比。相比之下，不加窗口化的基线仅保留 28% 和 8%，仅加窗口化不重基的保留 40% 和 44%。该方法在不同目标模型（密集 7B 和块稀疏模型）和多个语料库上均得到验证，且窗口大小参数在 32k 到 1M 范围内表现稳定。","reading_guide":"重点关注 Figure 2 对方法核心机制的直观解释，以及 Section 5 中关于 RoPE 索引重基保持计算图等效性的理论分析。Section 6.2 的实验结果（Figure 1）直观展示了该方法在超长上下文下的性能保持能力。预计阅读时间 15 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In ultra-long context scenarios (e.g., million tokens), the speedup of speculative decoding severely degrades or even becomes negative. This is because the attention cost of the drafter model scales linearly with the context length, making it no longer a 'cheap' approximation.","importance":"Challenges the prevailing assumption that long-context speculative decoding requires training dedicated long-context drafters. It introduces a plug-and-play, training-free approach that enables existing short-context drafters to maintain high efficiency at ultra-long contexts, significantly lowering the barrier for long-context inference optimization.","technical_approach":"The core insight is that existing drafters (like the EAGLE family) are 'target-conditioned': their inputs already include hidden states computed by the target model over the entire prefix. Thus, distant context information is already accessible to the drafter without needing its own global attention. Based on this, the authors propose two training-free interventions: 1) Windowing: Restricting the drafter's attention to the most recent W tokens, making its cost independent of the total context length N. 2) Index Re-basing: Mapping the drafter's RoPE indices back into its trained finite range [0, Pmax) using modulo arithmetic, preventing silent failures caused by index out-of-bounds. These are implemented as a pure inference-time patch in vLLM.","experiments":"At a 2M token context, using frozen short-context drafters with both windowing and index re-basing retained 90% and 119% of their 8k speedup. In contrast, unwindowed baselines retained only 28% and 8%, and windowed-only baselines retained 40% and 44%. The method was validated across different target models (dense 7B and block-sparse) and multiple corpora, with the window size parameter showing stability from 32k to 1M.","reading_guide":"Focus on Figure 2 for a clear visual explanation of the mechanism, and Section 5 for the theoretical analysis of how RoPE index re-basing preserves the computation graph. The experimental results in Section 6.2 (Figure 1) clearly demonstrate the method's ability to maintain performance at ultra-long contexts. Estimated reading time: 15 minutes.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改你对 speculative decoding 的一个默认前提：长上下文不一定要长 drafter；先看方法假设是否 target-conditioned，再看吞吐曲线和 acceptance rate 就够了。","en":"This changes a default assumption in speculative decoding: long context may not need a long-context drafter; first check the target-conditioned assumption, then the throughput and acceptance-rate curves."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Aref Jafari","Vansh Bhatia","Matt Qin","Subhajit Dutta Chowdhury","Ben Lee","Vikram Appia","Ali Ghodsi","Mehdi Rezagholizadeh"],"notable_authors":["Ali Ghodsi"],"tags":["speculative-decoding","long-context","serving","RoPE","inference-optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20865","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20865","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.20753","title":"Optimal Hydra: Model Heterogeneous Federated Learning for LLMs using Data-Informed Hydra-Nets","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) trained on vast amounts of data have demonstrated impressive performance over a range of tasks, but future performance gains will require increasing amounts of data and compute. Federated Learning (FL) facilitates training on decentralized private data, but client resource limitations can make training impractical, particularly as model sizes continue to expand. These resource constraints make the assumption of a shared server-client architecture impractical. Model h","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-14T18:08:34Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2610_20753.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体但常被回避的问题：当联邦学习参与方的算力、显存和可训练模型规模差异很大时，如何对大语言模型做联邦微调，而不是被迫要求所有客户端都持有同构全模型。已有 model-heterogeneous FL 多停留在预训练场景，通常用静态或随机子网，并强调参数应尽量均匀训练；但在 LLM 微调里，预训练模型已经提供了较强初始化，真正关键的是如何在不破坏全局聚合稳定性的前提下，让不同客户端保留与其本地数据分布更匹配的子结构。论文要解决的核心就是：面向微调而非预训练，设计一种数据感知的异构子网选择与聚合方法，在资源受限和数据异质同时存在时，兼顾性能、稳定性和资源开销。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接影响不在于提出新的预训练目标，而在于补上了 pretrain 到 decentralized post-train 之间的一段接口。它给出的信息有两点。第一，预训练阶段形成的通用表征会改变后续异构训练的最优策略，预训练里强调的“参数均匀覆盖”在微调里未必仍是必要条件。第二，子网结构如果能利用本地数据分布来定制，而不是随机裁剪，异构联邦微调的性能和资源效率都能明显改善。对关心 continual pretrain、私有数据增量训练、边端协同训练的人，这篇论文的价值在于提供了一个较完整的设计空间：哪些层需要全局共识，哪些局部结构可以因数据而异，以及这种划分如何影响聚合稳定性。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Optimal Hydra，核心对象是 Hydra-Net。整体流程分两步。\n\n第一步是 common layer selection。作者认为 Transformer 层承担较宏观的特征变换，如果客户端在这些层上分歧过大，会放大全局聚合时的结构不一致和 client drift。于是他们先让客户端协同识别一小部分“重要层”，这些层构成共享骨架，在这些层里不允许删除 attention head。文中这里借鉴并改造了已有的层重要性度量，使其能在联邦环境下估计哪些层更值得全体保留。这个设计不是简单追求参数公平，而是先固定一部分全局一致的表示通道，给后续异构训练留出稳定锚点。\n\n第二步是 individual head selection。对于其余“非重要层”，每个客户端根据本地数据，用梯度打分选择需要保留的 attention heads，其余 heads 被裁掉。这里的判断依据是 attention head 更偏任务相关、分布相关，适合做局部定制。这样得到的每个客户端子网共享同一层级骨架，但在部分层的 head 级结构上不同，形成 Hydra-Net。\n\n聚合方面，论文采用 direct index-mapping，把各客户端子网更新映射回全局模型原始参数索引后再聚合。这样避免了异构结构下常见的参数对齐困难。与早期静态/随机子网方法相比，区别有三点：一是子网不是随机给定，而是由数据驱动；二是结构异构主要落在 head 级，而不是任意粗暴裁剪；三是作者明确把“哪些参数必须被均衡训练”这个问题放到微调语境下重新审视，而不是沿用预训练结论。","experiments":"从全文可提炼出的主要结果有三类。\n\n第一类是总体效果。在较困难的 model-heterogeneous 条件下，Optimal Hydra 相比最强基线最高带来 42.5% 的性能提升。文中同时报告了资源收益：计算资源占用最高下降 32.0%，内存占用最高下降 29.3%。这说明它不是单纯用更多保留参数换性能，而是在更小子网下仍能提升效果。\n\n第二类是与子网选择策略相关的消融。作者比较了随机子网、静态子网、强调均匀参数训练的方案，以及他们的数据感知 Hydra-Net。结论是：在微调场景里，只要共享骨架足够稳定，局部不均匀的 head 保留并不会像预训练那样明显伤害收敛，反而因为更贴近本地分布而提升性能。这一点是全文最重要的经验结论。\n\n第三类是机制层面的分析。论文结合表示动态和 attention head 的任务相关性讨论，说明 common layers 更像全局共享表征通道，而 head 级裁剪更适合承载客户端特有模式。虽然摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，但从正文叙述看，作者做了不同异构强度、不同客户端资源条件下的对比，并展示了在资源越不均衡、数据越异质时，Hydra-Net 的优势越明显。若从研究角度看，最值得注意的不是单个 benchmark 数字，而是“预训练结论在微调里失效”的实验支撑。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合泛读，但有两个点值得认真看。\n\n先看方法部分里 Hydra-Net selection 的两步设计，尤其是 common layer selection 和 individual head selection 为什么分开做。这里体现了作者对 Transformer 结构粒度的判断：层负责全局一致性，head 负责局部适配。若只看一个图，优先看总流程图和子网选择示意图。\n\n再看实验里的消融和异构强度分析。重点不是绝对精度，而是比较“随机裁剪 vs 数据感知裁剪”“均匀训练假设是否仍成立”。如果时间有限，抓住三类表：总体性能表、资源占用表、子网选择消融表，20-30 分钟足够建立判断。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个。第一，层重要性度量在联邦环境中的鲁棒性如何，是否会受客户端数据偏斜影响。第二，head 级局部定制是否会在多轮聚合后形成隐性参数饥饿，即某些全局参数长期只被少数客户端更新。这两个问题决定方法能否推广到更大规模 continual 或 federated pretrain 场景。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tackles a practical but underexplored problem: how to fine-tune LLMs in federated settings when clients cannot host or train the same full model. Prior model-heterogeneous FL work mostly studies pre-training, often with static or random subnetworks, and typically argues for balanced parameter training. That assumption becomes questionable in the fine-tuning regime, where a pretrained model already provides strong representations. The real problem here is how to design client-specific subnetworks that match local data distributions, while preserving enough shared structure for stable global aggregation under severe resource heterogeneity.","importance":"Its value to pretraining-oriented researchers is indirect but real. paper asks how to turn judge feedback at test time into a controllable decoding intervention. Standard context optimization revises the system context, but it does not separately control how strongly the context edit should affect the next-token distribution or which token directions should be trusted.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to the interface between post-training, test-time compute, and verifier-guided generation. The useful idea is that a context edit can be viewed as a logit displacement inside a frozen model. By comparing the next-token distributions before and after the edit at the same prefix, the method converts textual feedback into prefix-dependent logit steering. This gives a concrete bridge between prompt refinement and decoding control, and it helps reason about why judge feedback can improve candidate generation rather than only answer selection.","technical_approach":"EMO alternates two steps. In the E-step, an external judge provides textual gradients that revise the question-specific system context, with no gold answer and no parameter update. In the M-step, the method evaluates the next-token distributions under the previous and revised contexts at a shared generated prefix. Their contrast defines a steering signal, which is applied as a confidence-constrained correction to the revised-context logits. The correction is meant to preserve the useful distributional shift induced by the context edit while limiting unstable or low-confidence directions. The paper also provides a logit-space interpretation: both parameter and context updates can appear as additive logit changes, but parameter updates change the model’s mapping for future contexts and prefixes, whereas EMO only uses finite displacements induced by context edits in a frozen model. Under context-improvement and faithful-steering assumptions, the one-round analysis shows improvement in target KL.","experiments":"The evaluation spans eight models and six benchmarks. EMO improves over the base setting in 39 out of 40 aggregated cases, with an average gain of +9.10 percentage points. Holding the judge and candidate count fixed, adding the M-step gives an additional +1.53 points on average over context-only optimization and improves MMLU-Pro for all eight models. Under matched target-model generation budgets, EMO achieves higher oracle coverage than independent sampling in seven of nine settings, suggesting that the method improves candidate generation, not only final answer selection. The ablations focus on the M-step, decoding choices, and budget-controlled comparisons.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–45 minutes. Through controlled evaluations on APEX-Agents and ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T10:05:54Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21624.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是一般意义上的“prompt robustness”，而是更贴近真实部署的 agent 交互鲁棒性：当任务目标、流程文档、历史上下文、工具接口这四类输入中的任意一类出现缺失、冗余或错误时，LLM agent 会怎样失效，失效发生在什么环节，以及现有评测为什么看不见这些问题。论文的关键点在于把 agent 失败从单轮输入扰动，扩展到完整交互闭环中的信息污染与误传播。","importance":"对预训练本身，它不是直接提出新的训练方法，但对“模型在脏信息环境中的稳健性”给了更细的失效分解，这对后续数据构造、continual pretrain、tool-use pretrain、以及 post-train 中的 robustness-oriented curriculum 都有参考价值。更具体地说，它说明 agent 的脆弱性并不只来自推理能力不足，而常常来自对输入来源缺乏校验、对上下文污染缺乏辨识、对工具契约变化缺乏适配。这类问题如果只靠 clean benchmark 很难暴露，因此这篇工作的价值主要在评测框架和失效机制刻画，而不是方法突破。","technical_approach":"方法上，论文提出 AgentPerturb，一个二维扰动框架。第一维是扰动目标：用户指令 U、程序/政策/技能文档 P、继承上下文 C、工具契约 T。第二维是扰动类型：Missing、Redundant、Incorrect。两者组合形成 12 个 perturbation family。设计上有两个值得注意的点。第一，它坚持 single-target edit，也就是每次只改一个信息源，其他保持不变，这样可以把性能下降更清楚地归因到具体输入通道，而不是多因素混杂。第二，它不是随便加噪，而是要求扰动后任务目标不变、环境仍可执行、原任务仍存在有效解路径，因此测到的是 agent 对信息污染的鲁棒性，而不是 benchmark 被破坏后的无意义失败。\n\n实例化上，作者把这套框架落在两个长程、可验证的 agent benchmark 上：APEX-Agents 和 Toolathlon。前者偏知识密集型专业工作流，后者偏复杂工具使用。论文还做了三层分析。Outcome level 看 clean 到 perturbed 的成功率下降；factor level 分析 target 和 type 哪个更主导，并考察与 clean performance、任务属性的关系；trajectory level 则人工审计失败轨迹，观察 reasoning、tool call、observation 和 state transition 中错误如何传播。全文最有信息量的结论之一是：敏感性更多按 target 组织，而不是按 perturbation type 组织。也就是说，问题更像“模型对哪类信息源最脆弱”，而不是“缺失/冗余/错误哪种噪声最危险”。这对训练设计很重要，因为它暗示鲁棒性增强可能需要按信息通道做专门建模，而不是统一加噪。","experiments":"实验覆盖 10 个前沿模型，在 APEX-Agents 和 Toolathlon 上比较 clean 与各类扰动后的表现。摘要里给出的总体结论是，所有扰动都会稳定拉低性能，但主导脆弱点会随环境变化：不同 benchmark 中，模型对 user utterance、procedural spec、context、tool contract 的敏感度排序并不相同。论文还特别分析了 Hunyuan-3-High，指出只看 task success 会掩盖 token efficiency 的明显恶化，也就是有些模型表面上还能做对，但会因为反复试错、无效调用、冗长推理而显著增加成本。\n\n在机制分析部分，作者人工审计了 30 个案例：DeepSeek-V4-Flash 在 clean task 上成功，但在 perturbation 后失败。这里的观察很一致：agent 很少主动质疑被改动的信息，也很少做交叉验证；一旦早期接受了污染输入，后续会把错误当成事实继续传递到计划、工具调用和状态更新里。这个结果虽然不是大样本统计，但对理解 agent failure mode 很有帮助。由于给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，无法逐项复述各模型在 12 类扰动上的具体降幅；但从论文结构看，最值得看的不是单个分数，而是 target/type 分解、环境差异，以及 trajectory audit 中的共性失效模式。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，理解为什么要用“target × type”而不是只做 prompt noise。然后看方法部分对 U/P/C/T 与 Missing/Redundant/Incorrect 的定义，确认作者如何保证 perturbation 后任务仍可解。接着重点看结果里关于 factor-level analysis 的图表，尤其是 target 比 type 更能解释脆弱性的证据。最后看 trajectory analysis 和 30 个案例审计，这部分最能帮助判断后续训练该往哪里发力。\n\n如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟可以抓住主线。若从预训练或 post-train 角度阅读，建议带着三个问题看：一，哪些信息源最值得在训练中显式加噪；二，模型失败是因为能力不足，还是因为缺少“怀疑输入”的机制；三，token efficiency 的退化能否作为鲁棒性训练的辅助目标，而不只看最终成功率。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies robustness of LLM agents in realistic interaction loops rather than standard prompt perturbation. The core question is: when one information channel in an agent task is corrupted—user request, procedural documents, carried-over context, or tool contract—how do current agents fail, where does the failure propagate, and why do clean benchmark evaluations miss this behavior?","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to foundation-model research because it exposes a class of failures that clean training and clean evaluation usually hide. The contribution is mainly an evaluation and failure-analysis framework. For pretraining and post-training, the practical implication is that robustness is not only about stronger reasoning. It also depends on whether the model can detect contaminated context, question inconsistent inputs, and adapt to interface drift. Those signals matter for data construction, tool-use pretraining, robustness curricula, and continual training under noisy environments.","technical_approach":"$22","experiments":"$23","reading_guide":"Start with the introduction and the main framework figure to understand why the paper moves beyond prompt noise into interaction-level perturbations. Then read the method section carefully, especially the definitions of U/P/C/T and Missing/Redundant/Incorrect, and how the perturbation pipeline preserves solvability. However, studies use different rating scales, and whether their scores remain comparable across scale designs is understudied. To address this gap, we define and systematically investigate scale sensitivity, the variation in LLM-generated scores caused by rating scale design. We distinguish numerical sensitivity, changes in normalized scores, from sem","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T16:42:15Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22187.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","math.NA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是：当同一段文本、同一个模型、同一个任务，只改变评分量表的设计时，LLM 给出的标量分数会不会发生系统性漂移，以及这种漂移会不会改变结论解释。作者把这个问题拆成两层：一层是 numerical sensitivity，即不同量表下归一化后的数值是否稳定；另一层是 semantic sensitivity，即这些数值变化是否会把文本从一个语义类别推到另一个类别。核心不是做一个新的打分模型，而是检验“LLM 作为标量测量器”这一用法本身是否稳健。","importance":"对基座研究本身，这篇论文不直接推进预训练方法、数据或优化，但它对评测和模型使用方式有一个很具体的提醒：LLM 输出的数字不是天然可比较的。很多研究把模型当作 judge、rater 或 latent construct estimator，用不同的 prompt 和不同的分值区间直接比较结果；这篇工作说明，量表设计本身就会引入可观偏差，而且这种偏差不只是线性缩放误差，还会改变语义判定。对做预训练和 post-train 的人，这意味着如果把 scalar supervision、reward modeling、rubric scoring、synthetic labeling 接到训练闭环里，量表定义会成为训练信号质量的一部分，不能当成无关实现细节。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者做的是一个控制变量很严格的全因子实验。评分量表有三个设计维度：格式分为 continuous 和 ordinal；范围分为 3、5、11、21 点；符号分为 signed 和 unsigned。三者组合得到 16 个量表条件。任务选了三个语义性质不同的文本评分任务：政治倾向、情感、毒性。每个任务都使用有人类标注的数据集。模型覆盖了闭源和开源系统，包括 GPT、Gemini、DeepSeek、Qwen、GLM。提示词方面比较了 zero-shot 和 rubric-based 两种设置，其中 rubric prompt 会把目标构念拆成多个维度，让模型先分维度打分，再给一个整体分数；但分析时只取最终总分，以保证两种 prompt 可比。\n\n关键设计点在于 sensitivity 的定义方式。作者不是简单比较不同条件下的原始分数，而是对不同量表做归一化，然后只比较“恰好改变一个量表因素”的条件对。这样可以把格式、范围、符号三种因素的影响拆开。semantic sensitivity 则进一步把连续分数映射到语义类别，检查类别是否翻转。这个定义很合理，因为很多社会科学或评测工作最终关心的不是 0.61 和 0.69 的差，而是“中立还是偏正”“低毒还是高毒”这类解释层面的稳定性。\n\n从全文看，作者的一个重要发现是：rubric-based prompting 并没有像很多人直觉上期待的那样显著降低 sensitivity。也就是说，把评分过程写得更结构化，不等于模型内部标尺就更稳定。另一个值得注意的点是 signed vs. unsigned 的影响尤其大，说明模型并不是先形成一个稳定的潜在连续表示，再做简单线性投影到不同量表；它更像是在 prompt 指定的数轴语义上重新构造判断边界。","experiments":"论文的主结果很集中，也比较容易记：当只改变一个量表设计元素时，归一化分数平均会移动大约 0.10，约五分之一文本会发生语义类别变化。这个量级已经足以影响很多基于阈值、分组统计或相关分析的结论。\n\n任务层面，toxicity 的 sensitivity 最大，尤其对 sign 最敏感。这个现象有解释空间：毒性判断往往带有更强的规范性边界，signed scale 会引入“负向—中性—正向”式的对称结构，而 unsigned scale 更像“无毒到高毒”的单边严重度刻画，两者不是简单重参数化。情感和政治倾向也有 sensitivity，但相对没那么剧烈。\n\n模型层面，作者比较了多家模型，结论不是某一个模型独有的问题，而是跨模型普遍存在。prompt 层面，rubric-based 没有稳定优于 zero-shot，这一点很关键，因为它否定了一个常见工程假设：只要把 rubric 写细，量表不一致的问题就会自然消失。\n\n虽然给出的全文片段没有完整表格数字，但从引言和实验设计部分已经能确认几个核心量化结论：16 个量表条件、3 个任务、2 类 prompt、5 个模型；主效应量是约 0.10 的归一化分数偏移和约 20% 的类别翻转率。对这类 measurement robustness 论文来说，这两个数字就是最该记住的结果。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不需要逐页精读。先看 Introduction，抓住作者如何定义 numerical sensitivity 和 semantic sensitivity。然后直接看 Experimental Design，重点理解 16 为什么最敏感；4）rubric prompting 为什么没有带来预期中的稳定性提升。对做 LLM-as-a-judge、reward data、synthetic labeling 的人，最值得深挖的是 sign effect，因为它提示 prompt 中数轴语义会直接改变模型的判别边界。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a narrow but important question: if you keep the text, task, and model fixed, and only change the rating scale design in the prompt, how much do LLM-generated scalar scores move, and do Yet different questions may require different visual operations and processing depths. We present AdaLoop, a framework for question-conditioned visual recurrence in VLMs. AdaLoop adapts visual computation to the question and reuses the encoder's transformations across multiple iterations, with th","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T06:14:16Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21323.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有 VLM 通常把视觉编码器当作一次性、与问题无关的特征提取器。AdaLoop 试图让视觉编码器根据问题进行多轮视觉计算，而不是把全部推理压力交给语言模型。","importance":"这项工作对 VLM 架构和训练范式有一定启发。它把推理时可变计算量引入视觉侧，而不是只扩展语言侧 CoT 或增加视觉 token。对预训练研究的启发在于：冻结的视觉编码器并不只能提供静态特征，通过问题条件化和权重复用，它的中间变换可以被重新组织成迭代视觉推理模块。这也提示未来 native multimodal pretraining 可以显式训练视觉侧 recurrent computation，而不是仅靠更大的 decoder 吸收视觉推理。","technical_approach":"AdaLoop 将视觉编码器分成 prelude、loop body 和 coda。prelude 先处理图像得到初始视觉状态；loop body 是一段连续视觉层，跨迭代共享权重，并在每一轮更新视觉状态；coda 和连接器把每轮状态转成语言模型可读的视觉 token。原始视觉权重保持冻结，问题条件化通过 AdaLN 完成：从问题 embedding 中池化出条件向量，再为每个视觉 block 的 attention 和 MLP 子层预测 shift、scale 和 residual gain。初始化为零，使训练开始时尽量保留原始编码器行为。作者还比较了语言模型使用多轮视觉状态的方式，包括只读当前状态、累积状态，以及把状态与答案草稿交错输入。训练目标仍是相同的最终答案，因此推理时可以按任务难度调整视觉循环次数。","experiments":"作者构建了 VisRO，用程序化方式生成视觉推理任务，包括分组、计数、连通性、空间追踪、遮挡/补全等，答案可自动验证，难度和分布移位可控。在 Qwen2.5-VL、PaliGemma 2、LLaVA-1.5 三类 VLM 上，AdaLoop 在相同训练数据和优化步数下优于标准微调和问题条件化单次前向基线。问题条件化本身已带来较强单次基线；加入循环后，VisRO in-distribution 准确率再提升 2.8 到 4.6 个百分点，相对标准微调总提升约 11 到 15 个百分点。在 Qwen2.5-VL 上，与参数量匹配、训练 FLOPs 近似匹配的更长训练单次前向基线相比，AdaLoop 的 VisRO 表现有竞争力，同时保留更好的通用 VQA 能力。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 分钟。先看方法部分的 prelude / loop body / coda 拆分，以及 AdaLN 如何作用在冻结视觉层上，这是主要设计点。然后看三种多轮视觉状态喂给语言模型的方案，理解为什么循环不只是重复编码图片。实验部分重点看 VisRO 的任务设计和分布外设置，因为它决定了结果是否真在测视觉计算。消融实验中，单纯 loop 冻结层收益有限、AdaLN 与 loop 互补，这部分最值得关注。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a limitation of common VLMs: the vision encoder processes the image once, independently of the question, and the language model is left to perform most of the reasoning. AdaLoop asks whether a pretrained vision encoder can be reused as a question-conditioned iterative visual computation module.","importance":"The contribution is relevant to multimodal architecture design rather than language-model pretraining in the narrow sense. Its useful signal is that inference-time adaptive compute can be placed on the visual side, not only in language-side chain-of-thought or longer decoding. Frozen vision encoders can be steered and reused through conditioning and recurrence, suggesting that future native multimodal pretraining may benefit from explicitly training recurrent visual computation instead of relying only on a larger decoder.","technical_approach":"AdaLoop partitions the vision encoder into a prelude, a loop body, and a coda. The prelude maps the image to an initial visual state. The loop body is a span of consecutive encoder layers reused across iterations with tied original weights. The coda and connector convert each updated visual state into visual tokens for the language model. The original vision weights are frozen. Question conditioning is introduced through adaptive layer normalization: a learned attention pooler summarizes the question embeddings, and per-block modules predict shifts, scales, and residual gains for attention and MLP sublayers. Zero initialization preserves the original encoder behavior at the start of training. The paper also studies how the language model should consume the sequence of visual states: current-state reading, state accumulation, and interleaving visual states with answer drafts. All iterations use the same final-answer target, which allows adaptive visual compute at inference time.","experiments":"The authors introduce VisRO, a procedural suite for vision-centric reasoning tasks such as grouping, counting, connectivity, spatial tracing, and amodal perception. The dataset provides verifiable answers, controllable difficulty, and controlled distribution shifts. Experiments cover Qwen2.5-VL, PaliGemma 2, and LLaVA-1.5. With matched training data and optimizer updates, AdaLoop outperforms standard fine-tuning and question-conditioned single-pass baselines. Question conditioning creates a strong single-pass control; adding recurrence gives another 2.8 to 4.6 points of in-distribution VisRO accuracy, for a total gain of roughly 11 to 15 points over standard fine-tuning. On Qwen2.5-VL, under matched parameter count and approximately matched training FLOPs, AdaLoop is competitive on VisRO while preserving better general VQA capability than longer-trained single-pass controls.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30 minutes. Focus first on the prelude / loop body / coda decomposition and the AdaLN conditioning mechanism, since these are the main architectural choices. Then read the comparison of ways to expose iterative visual states to the language model; this clarifies why the method is more than repeated image encoding. In the experiments, pay attention to the VisRO task construction and distribution-shift settings, because they determine whether the benchmark is measuring visual computation rather than language priors. The most informative ablations are the ones showing that naive looping of frozen layers is weak, while AdaLN and recurrence provide complementary gains.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 VisRO 设计和 recurrence ablation；它提醒 VLM 预训练别把视觉塔默认当静态 tokenizer。","en":"Read the VisRO setup and recurrence ablations; the useful prior shift is that the vision tower need not remain a static tokenizer."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Harvard University","MIT","Affiliation:Harvard University MIT"],"authors":["Xinran Nicole Han","Yiyang Lu","Katelyn Gan","Todd Zickler"],"notable_authors":["Todd Zickler"],"tags":["VLM","adaptive-computation","vision-encoder","visual-reasoning","multimodal-architecture"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21323","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.21323","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.21614","title":"Qwen-Audio-Jev: A Unified Interface for Audio Decisions","abstract":"Large audio language models (LALMs) have made substantial progress in audio understanding. It shows that visual token budget, image fidelity, and evidence presentation materially affect whether a model can read document evidence. For VLM pretraining and mid-training, the implication is that high-resolution document understanding should not be treated only as a data-scale problem; the visual input interface and evidence format matter.","technical_approach":"The framework is organized around two requirements. Loud means using the generator’s visual-token capacity effectively, through both larger page-level token budgets and higher visual fidelity. The key observation is that token count alone does not explain the gains: repeated pages, repeated blank pages, and spatial tiling do not match full-page scaling. Clear means making relevant evidence easier to consume. The paper compares whitening irrelevant content, cropping regions, regrouping regions, and adding OCR. The most reliable design is to keep the full page in the input and add OCR from selected regions. LoC is training-free: it scores regions using retriever token embeddings, keeps the page-level visual context, and appends OCR from selected evidence regions.","experiments":"The main diagnosis uses SciEGQA oracle pages while fixing the retrieved pages, generator, prompt, decoding, and evaluation. With the same correct pages and generator, increasing the page-only budget from 1K to 32K improves accuracy by 15.22 points. At a fixed 16K budget, adding ground-truth crops improves accuracy by 3.75 points over page-only input. Without ground-truth annotations, LoC outperforms eight prior methods on SciEGQA, ViDoRe-v3, and MMLongBench-Doc. Compared with the ColQwen3 baseline using the same retriever and generator, it gains 8.88, 3.09, and 4.22 points respectively. However, existing approaches mainly emphasize the modeling capacity of LLMs while directly serializing images into pixel or residual sequences, leaving heterogeneous signal patterns entangled within a single prediction stream. In this paper, we argue that effective entropy modeling depends not only on model capacity, but also on the predictability ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T17:12:45Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22232.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题，不是单纯把更大的 LLM 搬到无损压缩里，而是更具体的一点：现有 LLM-based lossless image compression 多在像素域或残差域直接做自回归概率建模，导致不同频率、不同方向、不同局部统计性质的信号被揉进同一条预测流里。模型容量再大，也要不断在差异很大的统计分布之间切换，概率估计会变难。论文的核心主张是，熵模型的效果不仅取决于模型强不强，也取决于输入表示是否更可预测；因此应先把图像变成更结构化、更同质的表示，再让 LLM 去做概率建模。","importance":"对基座研究的直接启发不在图像压缩本身，而在“表示先行，再谈建模”的思路。它提醒人们，序列建模性能不能只归因于 backbone 容量，输入空间的统计整形同样关键。这个判断对 tokenizer、离散化、跨模态统一建模、音频/视觉 token 设计都有参考价值。论文还给出一个较完整的范式：固定可逆变换把原始信号拆成若干统计更稳定的子问题，再用共享大模型加轻量条件化去覆盖不同子分布。这和多模态预训练里常见的‘统一 backbone + modality/task prompt + 专用头’有相通之处。贡献量级上，它更像一个扎实的建模与接口设计案例，而不是会改变 LLM 预训练主线的工作。","technical_approach":"方法由三部分组成。第一部分是波域重表示。输入 8-bit RGB 图像先做可逆颜色变换到 YCoCg，再做四层固定 5/3 integer wavelet transform，得到 13 个 codec-defined subbands。这样低频结构和高频细节、不同方向分量被拆开，原来混杂的预测任务变成多个统计性质更清晰的子任务。第二部分是面向数值符号的 LLM 接口。作者没有把 wavelet coefficient 当普通离散 token 粗暴处理，而是设计了 coding-oriented numerical adaptation：对每个局部上下文中的系数值建立紧凑 local symbol space，把真实数值映射到局部 ID；同时引入 trainable value embedding，并用专门的 value prediction head 只在合法取值范围内做预测。这样做的原因很明确：预训练 LLM 擅长离散 token，但波域系数是数值型、范围随子带和上下文变化，若直接共享全局词表会造成词表稀疏、无效类别过多、数值邻近关系利用不足。第三部分是 prompt-based adaptive entropy coding。不同 subband 仍有明显分布差异，若每个子带单独训练模型，参数和部署成本都高。作者因此用任务提示把 subband identity、解码上下文等条件注入同一个共享 backbone，让一个 3B LLM 在不同子带间切换。整体上，概率分解仍遵循因果编码顺序，并最终接 arithmetic coding 完成无损压缩。方法上的关键取舍是：前端用固定可逆变换保证编码正确性和工程稳定性，学习部分集中在概率建模接口与条件化适配，而不是端到端学习整套 codec。","experiments":"主结果上，WaveLLM 在 Kodak、CLIC.pro、DIV2K 上分别达到 2.51、2.24、2.27 bpsp。论文强调它只用 3B backbone，却超过了近期 8B 级 LLM-based compressors，并且和强 learned lossless codecs 保持竞争力。对这类工作，bpsp 越低越好，因此这个结果说明改表示后，同等甚至更小模型容量可以得到更好的熵估计。消融的关键信息主要应看三类：一是域选择，像素域/残差域/波域的比较，用来支撑“输入可预测性”这个主张；二是数值接口设计，验证 local ID 映射、value embedding、dedicated value head 是否都必要；三是共享模型的 prompt 适配，相比每子带独立模型或不加 prompt 的共享模型，是否在压缩率和参数效率之间取得更好平衡。摘要里没有给出更细的消融数字，但从全文叙述看，作者不是靠单一大模型堆出来的提升，而是靠表示分解和接口设计共同带来的收益。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 和 Figure 1，理解作者为什么认为像素域和残差域都把异质统计混在一起，而波域能把问题拆开。第二，看 Method 里关于 numerical adaptation 的部分，这是全文最有方法含量的地方；重点弄清 local symbol space 怎么定义、为什么需要专门的 value head、它如何保证只预测合法系数。第三，看实验中的 ablation，确认收益究竟主要来自 wavelet decomposition，还是来自 prompt/shared model/interface 设计。预计 25 到 35 分钟能读完主线。如果只想判断是否对基座研究有迁移价值，优先看 Figure 2、概率建模接口描述，以及不同表示域的对比表。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not merely about using a larger LLM for lossless image compression. Its actual target is a representation problem in entropy modeling: prior LLM-based compressors usually serialize images in the pixel domain or residual domain, forcing a single autoregressive model to handle mixed statistics from low-frequency structure, high-frequency detail, and directional components all at once. The authors argue that entropy coding quality depends not only on model capacity, but also on how predictable the input representation is. Their goal is therefore to reorganize the signal into more homogeneous and structured components before applying an LLM-based probability model.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research comes from the modeling lesson rather than the compression task itself. The paper makes a clean point: sequence modeling quality should not be attributed to backbone scale alone; the statistical shape of the input space matters just as much. That is a useful lens for tokenizer design, discrete representations, multimodal tokenization, and audio/vision language modeling. It also presents a practical pattern: use a fixed reversible transform to split a hard heterogeneous prediction problem into simpler subproblems, then cover them with one shared large model plus lightweight conditioning. This resembles the “shared backbone + modality/task prompt + specialized head” recipe seen in multimodal pretraining. The contribution is solid and instructive, though not a central advance for mainstream LLM pretraining.","technical_approach":"$29","experiments":"$2a","reading_guide":"For a quick but useful read, focus on three parts. Start with the Introduction and Figure 1 to understand why the authors view pixel-domain and residual-domain serialization as statistically entangled, and why wavelet subbands make the prediction problem easier. Then read the numerical adaptation section in the Method carefully; this is where most of the method’s substance sits. Pay attention to how the local symbol space is defined, why a dedicated value head is needed, and how validity constraints are enforced. Finally, inspect the ablations to see whether the gains mainly come from the wavelet decomposition itself or from the prompting and interface choices. A solid skim should take about 25–35 minutes. If the goal is to judge transfer value for foundation-model work, prioritize Figure 2, the probability-modeling interface, and the representation-domain comparison tables.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将图像压缩转化为 LLM 的自回归生成问题，其 numeric tokenizer 的设计和将异构信号转化为同质子问题的思路，对多模态 tokenization 有一定参考价值。","en":"Transforms image compression into an LLM autoregressive generation problem. e","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T17:40:30Z","category_id":"multimodal_gen","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22282.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在线 RL 对齐 diffusion/flow 视觉生成器时，现有做法通常把“采样轨迹里的随机性”当作 action（或把 ODE sampler 改成 SDE），导致 rollout 成本高、实现侵入性强，并且会改变推理时的 sampler。论文要解决的问题是：能否在不改 sampler、不加额外 policy head 的前提下，引入可控的随机探索与可微的策略梯度信号，让训练学到的 reward GPU-hours 显著下降），更接近可规模化的在线对齐；2）训练时引入的随机性与推理时的确定性 sampler 解耦，减少“训练-推理不一致”带来的工程风险。对纯文本 LLM 预训练的启发是：把策略空间放到模型内部的结构性选择（routing/masking/conditional compute）上，可能比直接扰动生成轨迹更稳定、更省 rollout。","technical_approach":"RPO（Routing Policy Optimization）的核心是把 DiT/视频扩散模型里的注意力可见性当作 action。做法分三层：\n1）路由动作（route as action）：把视觉 token 按空间邻域分成 block。对少数选定的 denoising level，每个 query block 不再对所有 key block 做 dense attention，而是“保留自身 block + 额外采样若干 key block”，采样分布与该层原生 dense attention mass 成比例；也允许选择保持 dense attention 作为一个动作。这样 action 是离散的路由掩码，不需要额外 policy head。\n2）用速度场的高斯似然构造可优化的 score-function：路由会改变该步的 attention，从而改变预测的 velocity v_θ(x^t,t,c)。论文用“围绕 dense velocity 的高斯”给 routed velocity 一个 likelihood（等价于把 routed velocity 看作对 dense velocity 的随机扰动），从而得到对 θ 的可微 surrogate，同时 route 的采样提供 REINFORCE 所需的 log-prob。\n3）受控的两条 rollout 估计器降低方差：每次采样两条轨迹，除 route 外共享所有随机源/上下文，使两条轨迹的 reward 差可以作为 leave-one-out advantage，减少 credit assignment 噪声。训练时 route proposal 分支不回传梯度；更新作用在 dense velocity 网络参数上。推理时完全移除 routing mask，回到原本 dense attention + 原本确定性 sampler（如 DDIM/ODE）——论文的关键假设是“通过临时路由学到的 reward 梯度能迁移到 dense 模型”。","experiments":"论文覆盖 text-to-image 与 text-to-video：\n- 模型：Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium（T2I）与 Wan2.2-TI2V-5B（T2V）。\n- 主要对比：Flow-GRPO、DanceGRPO 等把确定性 sampler 随机化（ODE→SDE）或在轨迹上做随机动作的在线 RL 方法。\n- 结果（文中给出的关键数字）：\n  1）SD3.5 上，RPO 在 50 步训练中的前 25 步内就超过 Flow-GRPO 的最终 dense validation reward，并在结束时高出 1.1 分（reward 标度以文中定义为准）。\n  2）Wan2.2 上，RPO 在评估 checkpoint 中取得最强总体 VBench 分数，同时训练成本 24.1 GPU-hours；对比 Flow-GRPO 75.4、DanceGRPO 102.3 GPU-hours，对应 3.1× 与 4.2× 的成本下降。\n- 论文主张的机制证据主要来自：训练时路由、推理时 dense 的一致提升，说明更新确实落在 dense velocity 上而非“只对稀疏路由有效”。（更细的消融如路由层数/采样策略/两-rollout vs 单-rollout 的方差对比，需要以正文表格为准。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 里对“sampler-preserving online alignment”的动机与 Figure 1（两条 rollout 只在 route 上不同）来理解它为什么能降方差、降成本。\n- 精看 Method/Preliminaries 中三件事：\n  1）route 的定义：block 如何划分、在哪些 denoising level 开路由、route 的采样分布如何从 native attention mass 得到；\n  2）高斯 velocity likelihood 的形式：它如何把 routed transition 变成可打分的“动作”，以及梯度到底回到哪里（dense velocity 参数）；\n  3）两-rollout advantage 的构造：共享哪些随机源、如何做 replay scoring（文中提到“每个 routed transition 从记录的输入重打分，使后续 level 条件化于早期 route”）。\n- 最后看实验部分的成本核算口径（GPU-hours、训练步数、每步采样数/组大小）与推理时是否完全移除 routing，确认训练-推理接口是否干净。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Online RL for aligning diffusion/flow-based visual generators typically treats stochasticity in the reverse trajectory as the action (or turns an ODE sampler into an SDE), which is rollout-expensive and sampler-intrusive. The paper asks whether we can introduce explicit exploration without perturbing the sampler trajectory, and still obtain a tractable policy-gradient signal whose learned reward alignment transfers back to the original dense, deterministic inference path.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper per se, but it is highly relevant to the pretrain↔posttrain interface for large diffusion transformers: it proposes an internal, structural action space (attention routing) rather than trajectory noise as the policy. Practically, it targets lower-cost online alignment and avoids training–inference mismatch by removing routing at inference while keeping the original deterministic sampler. Conceptually, it suggests a broader pattern that may generalize to foundation models: put RL “actions” on conditional-compute / routing / masking decisions inside the network, instead of directly randomizing the generation dynamics.","technical_approach":"$2b","experiments":"Evaluations cover both text-to-image and text-to-video:\n- Models: Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium (T2I) and Wan2.2-TI2V-5B (T2V).\n- Baselines: sampler-space RL methods such as Flow-GRPO and DanceGRPO that inject stochasticity into the sampler dynamics.\n- Reported highlights:\n  1) On SD3.5, RPO surpasses Flow-GRPO’s final dense validation reward within 25 out of 50 training steps, and finishes 1.1 reward points higher.\n  2) On Wan2.2, RPO achieves the strongest overall VBench score among evaluated checkpoints with 24.1 GPU-hours, vs 75.4 for Flow-GRPO and 102.3 for DanceGRPO (3.1× and 4.2× lower cost).\n- The key empirical claim is transfer: training-time routing improves reward-aligned quality when evaluated with dense attention and the original deterministic sampler.","reading_guide":"Skim plan (30–45 minutes):\n- Start with the Introduction and the main figure illustrating the two-rollout control variate; Context distillation offers a promising path: privileged information extracted from experience is provided to a teacher, whose behavior is then distilled into a student model that has no access to it. However, both existing forms of context distillation have limitations. Off-policy context distillation trains the student on teacher-generated trajectories and thus suffers","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T07:39:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21427.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是部署后 LLM 如何从交互经验中持续学习。现有 context distillation 有一个两难：off-policy 方法让带 privileged context 的 teacher 生成完整修正轨迹，监督信号清楚，但训练状态来自 teacher，和学生推理时自己的状态分布不一致；on-policy 方法在学生自己的 rollout 上让 teacher 给监督，状态分布一致，但当学生从未走到正确行为附近时，监督很难把新行为注入进去。LoCD 的目标是在“低 exposure bias”和“能学到新行为”之间找到更好的训练位置。","importance":"这篇工作对 pretrain ↔ posttrain 闭环有较强启发。它不是简单提出一种 SFT 数据构造方式，而是在讨论部署经验如何参数化写回模型。这个问题会影响 continual pretrain、agentic RL、工具使用训练和长期在线改进。贡献的量级主要在训练范式：它把经验学习从“整条轨迹重写”或“完全跟随学生轨迹”改成“只在错误开始的局部片段插入 teacher 行为”。这个选择很关键，因为长轨迹学习的主要困难常常不是缺少反馈，而是反馈落在了不合适的状态分布上。","technical_approach":"LoCD 包含两步。第一步是 localized attribution：给定一次 interaction experience，即任务、学生生成的轨迹和环境或用户反馈，方法先定位需要修正的 critical span。这个 span 对应学生行为开始偏离的局部片段，并把相关 privileged context 归因到这个片段。第二步是 localized distillation：从学生自己的前缀出发，让拥有 privileged context 的 teacher 只重新生成这个短片段，然后用该片段训练没有 privileged context 的 student。这样做和 off-policy distillation 的区别是，LoCD 不让 teacher 接管整条轨迹，训练前缀仍然来自学生，因此状态分布更接近推理时。它和 on-policy distillation 的区别是，LoCD 不要求所有监督都落在学生已经生成的 token 上，而是在关键位置引入 teacher 生成的局部行为，因此可以直接教会学生当前分布之外的动作或推理步骤。论文还通过 controlled knowledge-injection 实验解释 on-policy 的瓶颈：答案越长，学习越呈现逐步推进的模式，因为后半段监督只有在学生先生成接近目标的前缀时才变得有效。这说明完全 on-policy 的 token-level 蒸馏会受 sequential learning bottleneck 限制。","experiments":"实验覆盖数学推理、代码生成、交互式决策和软件工程任务。相对最强基线，LoCD 在数学推理上提升 5.65%，代码生成提升 2.65%，WebShop 提升 5.60%，ALFWorld 提升 7.46%。这些结果说明它不只适用于单轮答案修正，也适用于多步环境交互。论文还报告了训练动态层面的差异：on-policy 和 off-policy context distillation 会出现持续的 policy entropy collapse，而 LoCD 的训练更稳定。进一步分析显示，LoCD 用更小、更稀疏的参数更新取得收益，支持“只监督关键片段”这一设计。可确认的主要数字是上述任务提升幅度；模型规模、训练样本量和具体 rollout 配置需要在实验设置表中核对。","reading_guide":"需要精读。先读 Introduction 中对 off-policy 与 on-policy context distillation 的失败模式分析，这是理解 LoCD span 定位是否依赖强 oracle 或任务特定反馈；二是局部 teacher 片段和学生后续 rollout 之间是否仍会产生残余分布偏移。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how an LLM can continually learn from interaction experience after deployment. Existing context distillation methods face a trade-off. Off-policy distillation gives clear supervision from teacher-generated revised trajectories, but trains on teacher states rather than student states. On-policy distillation avoids this mismatch by supervising student rollouts, but it struggles to teach behavior the student cannot yet produce. LoCD targets the narrow region where supervision is both informative and close to the student’s own state distribution.","importance":"The work is relevant to the interface between pretraining, post-training, and continual improvement. It treats deployment experience as something that can be written back into model parameters, rather than only stored in retrieval memory. The main contribution is a training paradigm: revise only the local segment where the trajectory starts to fail, instead of rewriting the whole trajectory or supervising only the tokens already produced by the student. This matters for long-horizon reasoning and agent learning, where the quality of the training state distribution often matters as much as the feedback itself.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"The experiments cover math reasoning, code generation, interactive decision-making, and software-engineering tasks. The gains are accompanied by more stable training dynamics. The paper reports that on-policy and off-policy context distillation can suffer sustained policy-entropy collapse, while LoCD avoids this pattern. Additional analysis shows that LoCD achieves its gains with smaller and sparser parameter updates, consistent with the idea that supervision should be concentrated on critical spans.
