Current loops such as R-Zero, Agent0, and Skill Self-Play make this decision through the task, admitting tasks that look learnable and inheriting every resulting update. We identify a selection-to-update gap in these loops, where a task that looks learnable need not produce a useful update. Most updates on verified tasks fail to make correct answers more likely, yet","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T15:07:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22029.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作抓住了自演化训练里一个很具体、但此前常被混过去的问题：任务筛选通过，不等于这次参数更新真的有益。R-Zero、Agent0、Skill Self-Play 这类循环通常先找“看起来可学”的题，再把由这些题产生的更新整体继承到下一轮。问题在于，任务侧信号只描述更新前的行为，比如通过率、分歧度、是否位于能力前沿；它并不直接告诉你，优化器走完这一步之后，模型是否真的更倾向于正确答案，还是只是把局部噪声、错误偏好、甚至已有能力的退化一起带进了下一轮。文中把这个问题定义为 selection-to-update gap，并且用审计结果说明它不是边角现象：在 Agent0 和 R-Zero 的原生 admission 中，42–52% 被接纳的任务在独立响应下并没有留下正向前沿收益；在 Skill-SP 的 96 次更新里，有 60 次对 correct-target NLL 的增益非正。也就是说，自演化系统真正需要决策的不是“哪些题值得做”，而是“哪些训练后状态值得继承”。","importance":"对预训练和自训练研究，这篇工作的价值不在于又提出一个 task generator，而在于把“更新是否值得保留”单独提升为一等问题。这个视角很重要，因为一旦训练是闭环的，错误继承会同时污染参数和后续课程分布，回合越多，漂移越难纠正。文中给出的贡献有三层。第一层是问题刻画：它明确区分了 task-side selection 和 state transition evaluation，指出前者不能替代后者，并给出理论说明，证明仅靠更新前行为无法识别更新价值。第二层是方法层面的修正：HeirCommit 用 fresh probes 对冻结后的 successor 做独立确认，避免把筛选时的偶然性直接当成继承依据；EvoHeir 进一步把“继承谁的参数”和“吸收谁的经验”拆开，允许弱 donor 提供有用经验，但不必把其参数状态整体传下去。第三层是经验层面的启发：经验可以比产生它的模型活得更久，甚至来自被拒绝轮次的参考解仍然能帮助后续 learner。这一点对 continual pretrain、self-training、RL 生成数据回流 pretrain 都有直接启发：不要把数据价值和参数价值绑死在同一个 acceptance decision 上。","technical_approach":"$2d","experiments":"$2e","reading_guide":"$2f","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"$30","importance":"$31","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"$34","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得立刻对照自训练/R-Zero 内部流程：把“是否继承更新”当成一等公民做 commit gate；先读误差分解那节和 commit 评测设计。","en":"Treat “inherit-or-revert” as a first-class commit gate in self-training loops; A single page-level advantage does not explicitly identify which elements differ in quality across sampled outputs or where their errors occur. We introduce Page-to-Token Policy Optimization (P2TPO), a structure-aligned credit-assignment framework that operates on full-page policy trajectories. Reference-element alignment ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T14:32:00Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21979.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"全页文档解析（图像→Markdown 序列）在 RL 后训练时，常用的 page-level reward/advantage（如 GRPO 的 response-level 系数）无法定位局部错误：不同采样输出可能总体分数接近，但一个漏表格、另一个错公式。论文要解决的是：在保持“整页自回归生成 + 页级评测”的前提下，把 reward 差异对齐到参考文档的具体元素（block/instance），并进一步把元素级 credit 分配到 token 级位置，尤其要处理缺失元素与“所有输出都同样不完美”导致的信号塌缩。","importance":"对基座预训练的直接影响有限（更偏文档解析的 post-train/RL credit assignment），但方法层面有两点可迁移：\n1）把 credit assignment 从 response-level 拆到“结构对齐后的局部单位”，本质是在做更细粒度的 advantage shaping，减少无关 token 的梯度噪声；\n2）显式处理 missing outcome（漏掉的元素没有 span）与 tied-but-imperfect（大家都错但程度不同难以拉开）这两类在长序列生成里很常见的 RL 痛点。若你的场景是长上下文生成、结构化输出、或任何需要局部可验证单元的 RL（代码 patch、表格、JSON），这套对齐+补全+再分配的框架有参考价值。","technical_approach":"P2TPO（Page-to-Token Policy Optimization）是一个在 GRPO 组采样框架上做“结构对齐 credit assignment”的方法，包含三步：\n1）Reference-element alignment：把预测与参考都解析成按阅读顺序排列的 block（文本/公式/表格等），用内容序列对齐允许插入删除，从而把每个生成 block 绑定到具体 reference instance y*_k，而不是绑定到生成序列位置。这样同类型多实例不会混。\n2）UAP（Unified Anchor Pricing）：把每个 reference 元素在不同 rollout 下的质量分 s_{i,k}（有上限 c_k）转成元素级系数，规则同时覆盖 matched 与 missing：\n- matched：对齐到生成 span 的 block 得到系数；\n- missing：整块缺失没有 span 时，通过“已有的边界 token/占位位置”走单独的 credit 路径，确保 omission 也能产生梯度；\n- tied-but-imperfect：当多个输出对某元素得分相同但都不满分时，引入相对固定的 quality ceiling（文中称 ceiling/anchor）来避免简单中心化后信号为 0。\n3）SCR（Span Credit Redistribution）：对 matched block，把元素系数在 block 内再分配到 token 级。负向系数更集中到对齐到错误的位置，正向系数更集中到正确位置；同时保持两个约束：符号不变、总 credit 质量守恒（论文给出守恒证明与“informative mask 何时能提高目标位置分配”的条件）。最终每个 token 的更新系数是：局部（元素/位置）项 + 共享 page-level 项，仍然保持整页自回归条件化与 GRPO 式更新。","experiments":"实验设置强调公平对比：共享同一个 supervised checkpoint、匹配 update budget、5 个 seed。\n- 任务与数据：6 个 benchmark、共 13 个 primary split，覆盖数字文档、扫描件、真实拍照页面等；输出为全页 Markdown（含 LaTeX 公式、HTML 表格）。\n- 对比方法：4 个 response-level baseline（文中提到 GRPO、以及维度归一化的 GDPO、表格路由 credit 的 CSPO 等相关线）。\n- 结果摘要：P2TPO 在所有 13 个 primary split 上优于四个 baseline；在 3 种不同模型/初始化设置下，39 个（split×setting）对比里有 37 个均值最好。论文的主张是：元素对齐 + 缺失处理 + block 内再分配，能同时提升完整性（减少漏块）与局部准确性（减少局部 corruption）。\n（具体指标定义与绝对数值需要以实验表格为准；引言未给出单项提升幅度的数字。）","reading_guide":"泛读建议（45–60 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 的 failure modes 图（Figure 1）理解为什么 page-level advantage 会抹平元素差异，以及 missing/tied 的问题长什么样。\n- 重点看 Method 里三块：reference alignment 的对齐规则；UAP 的“匹配/缺失/同分不满分”三类定价规则；SCR 的守恒性质与 mask 条件（这是方法最可迁移的部分）。\n- 实验部分建议只抓两类表：总体 13 split 的主表 + 消融（是否去掉 UAP、是否去掉 SCR、是否只做元素级不做 token 级）。如果要复现，优先确认 block 解析与对齐实现细节，因为这是 credit assignment 是否稳定的前提。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In full-page document parsing (image → a single long Markdown sequence), RL post-training often uses a page-level reward/advantage that is broadcast to all tokens. This fails when outputs have similar overall scores but different local errors (e.g., one misses a table while another corrupts a formula). The paper targets structure-aligned credit assignment: compare rollouts at the level of reference elements (instances), handle missing elements and tied-but-imperfect scores, and then allocate element-level feedback down to token positions without breaking full-page autoregressive generation.","importance":"The contribution is mainly in RL credit assignment for long structured generation rather than pretraining. Still, it offers transferable ideas for foundation-model post-training: (i) advantage shaping on aligned structural units reduces gradient noise versus response-level coefficients, and (ii) explicit treatment of missing outcomes and tied-but-imperfect cases addresses common failure modes in long-horizon generation (structured JSON, tables, code patches).","technical_approach":"$35","experiments":"The evaluation emphasizes controlled comparisons (same supervised checkpoint, matched update budgets, 5 seeds):\n- Data/benchmarks: 6 benchmarks, 13 primary splits spanning digital documents, scanned pages, and real-world photos; output is full-page Markdown with LaTeX formulas and HTML tables.\n- Baselines: four response-level methods (including GRPO and related variants such as GDPO/CSPO mentioned in the paper).\n- Results: P2TPO outperforms all four baselines on all 13 primary splits; across three model/initialization settings, it achieves the best mean in 37 of 39 split×setting comparisons. mus","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T00:31:18Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21040.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文处理开放式专家任务中的自生成奖励问题。数学和代码任务可以用最终答案、执行结果或单元测试检查，但临床报告、法律分析、金融建议这类开放式任务没有天然答案 生成指令。每个任务包含请求 x 和 rubric R={(c_j,p_j)}，其中 c_j 是评分准则，p_j 是 [-10,10] 内非零权重，正权重奖励应包含内容，负权重惩罚错误或风险。核心循环不是直接相信模型写的 rubric，而是先构造 honest answer 和 hack answer，让 adversary 尝试拿高分；如果 exploit 能击败或接近 honest answer，就修复 rubric，使正确、完整的回答重新占优。外层 meta-optimizer 把这些 exploit 汇总成对未来 rubric 写法的改进。与普通 proposer-solver RSI 相比，RRI 的新增点是奖励也在递归改进，而不是只改任务或 solver。","experiments":"论文报告，朴素模型生成的 question-rubric pair 近 99% i","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-14T19:15:36Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_20828.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 reasoning distillation 里的数据生成瓶颈。学生模型要学会推理，通常需要带有正确答案的高质量 chain-of-thought 数据。最常见做法是让教师从问题出发生成 forward CoT，再用 rejection sampling 保留答对的样本；问题是难题上采样成本很高。另一条路是给教师问题和答案，让它生成 reverse CoT，这样便宜很多，但会产生 post-hoc rationalization：推理过程只是对已知答案的事后解释，而不是从问题出发的可学习推导。论文的核心问题是，能否把便宜的 reverse CoT 转换成更接近 verified forward CoT 分布的数据，从而在较低教师成本下得到更适合蒸馏的推理轨迹。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，更接近 post-train / reasoning data construction，但对 pretrain-posttrain 接口有现实意义。原因很简单：高质量推理数据越来越像一种稀缺训练信号，而不是随手可得的副产物。CoTranslator 给出的启发是，数据生成不一定非要直接从目标分布昂贵采样，也可以先从便宜但有偏的分布采样，再学习一个分布校正器。这个思路对合成推理数据、RL 前的数据准备、甚至某些 curriculum 设计都有参考价值。它的贡献量级主要体现在成本-效果折中：在明显更低的 teacher compute 下，保留了大部分 rejection sampling 的收益，并且比直接用 reverse CoT 或语义抑制类方法更稳。","technical_approach":"方法的关键是把 reverse CoT 到 verified forward CoT 的差异显式建模成一个 translation 问题，而不是继续在 prompt 上做约束。\n\n设问题为 q，正确答案为 a。Forward CoT 分布是教师仅看 q 生成推理；rejection sampling 从中筛出最终答案等于 a 的样本。Reverse CoT 分布则是教师在输入里同时看到 (q, a) 后生成推理。作者认为学生真正需要学习的是 rejection-sampled forward CoT 的分布，而不是 reverse CoT 的分布。\n\n于是他们先构造一个小规模配对集。对同一个问题，采一个 reverse CoT 响应 x，再从 forward CoT 多次采样，保留第一个答对的响应 z，形成 (x, z) 配对。然后训练一个 translator G_phi，用 teacher forcing 从 x 预测 z。一个重要设计是：translator 只看 reverse CoT 文本 x，不额外输入问题 q 或答案 a。这样做有两个目的。第一，和文中的分布推导保持一致，目标是学习从 source response 到 target response 的映射。第二，避免模型直接走 question-answer bypass，继续利用显式答案信息投机。\n\n训练好 translator 后，大规模数据构造时每个问题只需一次 reverse teacher call 得到 x，再一次 translator call 得到 z_hat，最后用 (q, z_hat) 训练学生。相比 rejection sampling 可能需要多次 forward teacher call 才拿到一个可用样本，这个流程把高成本部分压缩成一次便宜采样加一次较小模型翻译。\n\n与已有 semantic suppression 或结构约束方法相比，区别在于它不试图让 reverse CoT 自己“少泄露答案”，而是直接学习一个目标分布映射器。论文附录还给了 distribution recovery 相关分析，说明在一定条件下 translator 输出分布可以逼近 rejection-sampled forward 分布。","experiments":"实验覆盖六个学生模型和六个数学 benchmark，并做了三个迁移设置：迁移到新数学数据集、换教师模型、迁移到 STEM 题目。摘要和正文给出的关键数字有几项。\n\n第一，效果对比。对 Qwen3-4B-Base 这类 4B 学生，CoTranslator 相比 semantic suppression 平均准确率最高提升 5.82 个百分点。说明仅仅抑制答案泄露不够，显式分布翻译更有效。\n\n第二，成本-性能折中。两个 4B 学生在仅用 rejection sampling 估算算力的 35.9% 时，保留了其平均准确率的 95.0%。这是全文最核心的结果，因为它直接回答了“为什么不用 RS 直接生成”的问题：RS 更干净，但太贵；CoTranslator 用较小配对集训练 translator 后，可以在大规模生成阶段显著降本。\n\n第三，泛化。translator 不重训即可迁移到新数学数据集、不同教师和 STEM 领域。4B 和 8B 学生在 STEM 上平均分别提升 4.88 和 4.31 个百分点。这个结果说明 translator 学到的不是某个数据集模板，而是某种从 answer-conditioned rationale 到 question-grounded rationale 的改写规律。\n\n第四，分析实验。作者检查了生成结果与 RS 响应的相似度，以及 answer-dependent shortcuts 的减少情况。结论是 CoTranslator 生成的数据在分布上更接近 RS，同时比原始 reverse CoT 更少依赖已知答案痕迹。这部分虽然不是最终任务分数，但对理解方法为何有效很关键。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读偏精读。先看方法部分对三种分布的定义：FCG、RCG、RS。只有把目标分布想清楚，才知道作者为什么把问题写成 translation，而不是普通数据过滤。随后看 paired data construction 和 translator 训练目标，尤其注意 translator 不输入 q 和 a 这一设计，这里是方法成败的关键。\n\n实验部分优先看三类表：一是与 RS、RCG、semantic suppression 的主结果对比；二是 compute cost 对比；三是 transfer 实验。若时间有限，30-40 分钟可以抓住主线。若要深挖，建议看附录里的 distribution recovery 推导，以及分析实验中关于 shortcut 的定量指标。\n\n阅读时可以带着两个问题。第一，translator 学到的是“风格改写”还是“因果上更接近 forward reasoning 的结构变换”；第二，这种方法在数学之外是否仍成立，尤其在答案验证器不可靠、推理空间更开放的任务上会不会退化。前者关系到蒸馏数据质量，后者关系到方法外推边界。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a central bottleneck in reasoning distillation: generating large amounts of high-quality chain-of-thought data with verified correctness. Standard forward CoT generation plus rejection sampling is reliable but expensive, especially on hard problems where many teacher samples may be needed before one is accepted. Reverse CoT generation is much cheaper because the teacher sees the correct answer, but that creates post-hoc rationalization and a train-test mismatch: the student must reason without answer access at inference time. The core question is whether cheap reverse CoTs can be transformed into data that better matches the distribution of verified forward CoTs.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for the pretrain-to-posttrain interface because reasoning data is increasingly a scarce training signal. The main contribution is methodological: instead of sampling directly from an expensive target distribution, the paper samples from a cheaper but biased source distribution and learns a translator toward the desired one. That idea is relevant beyond math distillation. It suggests a general recipe for synthetic reasoning data construction, and more broadly for situations where high-quality supervision is expensive but a lower-quality proxy is cheap. The practical contribution is a favorable cost-quality trade-off: much lower teacher compute while retaining most of the benefit of rejection-sampled forward CoTs.","technical_approach":"$40","experiments":"$41","reading_guide":"$42","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得看数据生成成本曲线和失败样例；若 paired set 很小仍稳，内部数学蒸馏管线应复现。","en":"Worth reading for the data-cost curve and failure cases; if the small paired set is enough, this should be reproduced in an internal math-distillation pipeline."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zinan Tang","Zhuoshi Pan","Kairui Chen","Ze Yan","Shaomian Zheng","Yukun Zhang","Gang Wang","Biqing Huang"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reasoning-distillation","chain-of-thought","synthetic-data","math-reasoning","post-training"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20828","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20828","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.21404","title":"Multi-Reward Hacking: From Aggregation-Induced Drift to Trust-Anchored Defense","abstract":"To improve task performance while encouraging desirable response properties, LLM post-training often combines a primary reward (e.g., correctness) with auxiliary rewards (e.g., brevity or efficiency). However, how these rewards are aggregated can critically affect training outcomes. We show that symmetric aggregation, which treats reward channels equally, can induce a failure where aggregate reward improves while primary-task performance degrades, even when individual rewards are meaningful. We ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T07:21:58Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21404.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"多奖励后训练里，常把“主任务正确性”与“长度/格式/效率”等辅助奖励聚合成一个标量再做 RL（如 GRPO）。论文要解决的问题是：即使每个 reward channel 本身都合理，聚合规则也可能系统性地把策略推向“更容易拿到的辅助奖励”，导致 aggregate reward 上升但主任务性能崩溃；这种失败何时必然发生；以及如何设计一种结构性聚合/门控来避免这种 drift。","importance":"对 post-train 但与 pretrain 研究接口很直接：\n1) 现实训练里多 reward 越来越常见（verifier + 风格/长度/成本），而“聚合”往往被当作工程调参。论文给出一个可证明的失败模式，解释为什么“调权重”经常只能延缓崩溃。\n2) 结论对 scaling 也敏感：文中强调某个权重在一个模型尺度安全，在另一个尺度会失败。这对大规模持续训练/对齐流水线是硬问题。\n3) 提出的 TAG（Trust-Anchored Gating）把“优先级”写进聚合结构，而不是靠对称加权；这类结构性约束更像训练 recipe 的一部分，能减少 reward hacking 类不稳定性。","technical_approach":"1) 失败模式形式化：multi-reward hacking\n- 设置主奖励 r_p（正确性）与辅奖励 r_a（brevity/latency/格式等）。关键假设是：辅奖励更“可靠/更容易获得”（例如不解题也能写得短）。\n- 研究“对称聚合”（symmetric aggregation）：把各 channel 等价对待，并且对“单通道成功”给正向信用（例如只拿到 r_a 也能让聚合 reward 变好）。\n- 在一个 one-step 随机决策模型里，作者证明：在上述不平衡下，确定性的 conditional-mean GRPO 动力学会从任意初始化收敛到“偏向辅助”的行为，即使初始策略更偏主任务。\n- 他们进一步刻画何时这构成 reward hacking（相对未知 true reward）。\n\n2) 防御：Trust-Anchored Gating（TAG）\n- 两个子问题：优先哪个 channel；如何强制优先级。\n- 选 anchor：短暂地分别只优化每个 channel，测量 induced policy drift，选择 drift 最小的 channel 作为 anchor。直觉是：可被投机的奖励会诱导策略快速漂移到捷径行为。\n- 聚合结构：用 gate 打破对称性——“只拿到辅助成功”不比“双失败”更好；“主任务成功”与“主+辅都成功”仍区分开（后者更高）。\n- 理论保证：当主通道作为 anchor 且主任务成功仍可达时，TAG 使 conditional-mean GRPO 从任意初始化收敛到主任务偏好动作；并且对任意正的 gating weight 成立（相比调权重更稳）。\n\n3) 为什么不是简单 reweight\n- 论文明确展示：降低辅助权重只能延缓 collapse，不能从机制上阻止；并且安全权重不具备跨尺度可迁移性。TAG 把“辅助-only 不给额外信用”作为结构约束，直接切断了最常见的投机路径。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen 与 Llama 系列（1.7B–30B），任务包括数学与程序合成，辅助奖励既有兼容也有不兼容情形。\n- 现象复现：多种聚合规则下，辅助达成率趋近 1，aggregate reward 上升，但正确性/通过率下降到接近不解题的水平；并出现典型投机行为（例如为 brevity 输出极短猜测、为 latency 输出立即退出的程序、为符号检查而否定答案等）。\n- 与现有对称多目标方法对比：MO-GRPO、PG-OT 等“通道内再平衡但仍对称”的方法在他们的设置里也会同类崩溃。\n- TAG 的效果：在多数组合里显著缓解主任务退化，同时保留部分辅助收益。\n  - 文中给出的具体例子：Qwen3-4B 上加入 brevity reward 时，TAG 达到 0.581 pass@4（加性聚合仅 0.220），同时回复长度比 correctness-only 训练短 44%。\n- 还报告了“降低辅助权重延缓但不阻止”的实验，以及“某尺度安全、换尺度失败”的现象（对工程实践很关键）。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 40–60 分钟）：\n1) 先读理论部分对称聚合失败的条件与结论：重点抓住“辅助更易得 + 对称聚合给 auxiliary-only 正信用 ⇒ 动力学必然偏辅助”。这比具体算法名更重要。\n2) 看 TAG 的两步：anchor 选择（用 drift 选）与 gating 结构（aux-only 不加分）。理解它如何从结构上打破对称性。\n3) 实验优先看：\n   - Figure 1/5 这类展示“aggregate 上升但 correctness 崩”的曲线；\n   - 不同聚合（加权和、归一化、MO-GRPO/PG-OT）与 TAG 的对照表；\n   - 典型投机样例（能快速建立直觉）。\n4) 如果你在做多 reward RL：建议把“跨模型尺度权重失效”的段落当作 checklist，反推自己 pipeline 里哪些超参不具备可迁移性。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In multi-reward post-training, practitioners often aggregate a primary reward (task correctness) with auxiliary rewards (brevity, format, latency, etc.) into a single scalar for RL (e.g., GRPO). The paper studies a systematic failure: even when each reward channel is individually meaningful, the aggregation rule itself can drive training toward “easy auxiliary wins”, increasing aggregated reward while collapsing primary-task performance. It asks when this drift is inevitable and how to design an aggregation mechanism that enforces a primary objective.","importance":"This is a post-training paper, but it directly impacts the pretrain↔posttrain loop because multi-signal optimization is now standard in large-scale alignment pipelines:\n- It provides a mechanistic explanation for why “just tune weights” often only delays collapse, rather than fixing it.\n- It highlights scale sensitivity: a weight that is safe at one model size can fail at another, which is a practical risk for continual training and model iteration.\n- The proposed Trust-Anchored Gating (TAG) encodes priority structurally (aux-only success gets no extra credit), which is closer to a robust training recipe than symmetric reweighting.","technical_approach":"$43","experiments":"$44","reading_guide":"Skim plan (40–60 minutes):\n- Start with the theoretical result: identify the exact conditions under which symmetric aggregation provably selects auxiliary-favoring behavior (the “aux is easier + aux-only gets credit” logic).\n- Read TAG’s two components carefully: (i) anchor selection via isolated-optimization drift, and (ii) the gating rule that removes credit for aux-only success.\n- In experiments, prioritize plots showing “aggregate reward up, correctness down” and the ablations comparing reweighting vs TAG.\n- If you run multi-reward RL in practice, pay attention to the section on scale-dependent weight failure; Rollout Routing Replay (R3) mitigates this mismatch by reusing expert selections from the rollout stage during training. Our analysis shows that routing replay introduces gradient bias by losing","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T11:09:10Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21682.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作处理 MoE 模型在 RL 训练中的 off-policy 问题：rollout 引擎和训练引擎一旦出现策略、数值或版本差异，同一条响应在训练时可能走到不同专家，导致 router drift，并改变专家级梯度归因。","importance":"对预训练和后训练接口有直接参考价值，尤其是 MoE 基座做 RLVR 或异步 RL 时的稳定性问题。常见的 token 概率比值修正只看输出分布，不知道 token 被哪些专家处理；R3 这类 routing replay 虽能固定路径，但会让当前训练 router 本该选择的专家收不到梯度。RDC 的价值在于把 routing disagreement 当成 off-policy staleness 信号，用过滤而不是重放来控制偏移，保留了训练时原生路由的学习信号。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Router Drift Control。对每条 rollout response，在 teacher forcing 下分别记录 rollout router 和当前 training router 的专家选择，并按 MoE layer 统计 expert-usage distribution。然后把层级差异聚合成 response-level router drift score。训练时只丢弃 drift 最大的一小部分样本，保留样本仍使用当前训练模型自己的 routing，不强行 replay rollout 路由。与 R3 的区别很关键：R3 固定 rollout 期间的专家路径，降低了路径不一致，但改变了当前策略下的梯度归因；RDC 接受训练 router 的当前选择，只把高漂移样本视作过期或不可靠轨迹排除。它也可以和 TIS 组合，TIS 修 token 概率层面的 off-policy，RDC 修 routing 层面的 off-policy。","experiments":"实验覆盖同步 RL 和 fully asynchronous RL，重点观察 policy staleness 增强后的收益。论文报告在异步训练中，RDC 与 TIS 组合后，在 AIME 2024 和 AIME 2025 上的平均 peak validation accuracy 比单独 TIS 高 5.5 个点，比 R3 高 4.5 个点，并保持更稳定的训练曲线。消融的主要信息是：routing disagreement 不是均匀分布在所有样本上，少量高 drift response 对训练破坏更明显；简单 replay routing 会减少 rollout-training path mismatch，但会引入专家梯度偏置；按 response 过滤比 token 概率修正更能覆盖 MoE 特有的失配源。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 25–35 分钟。先看 Introduction 的三类 off-policy 来源和 Figure 1/2，明确为什么 MoE 比 dense 模型更容易受 rollout-training mismatch 影响。Method 部分重点看 router drift score 的定义、layer-wise expert usage 的聚合方式、过滤比例如何设置，以及 RDC 与 TIS/R3 的训练流程差异。实验部分重点看异步 RL 设置、AIME 2024/2025 曲线、RDC+TIS 对比 TIS 和 R3 的表格。值得深挖的是 R3 的梯度偏置分析：它提醒 MoE RL 不能只追求路径一致，还要看哪些专家在当前策略下应当获得学习信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies off-policy degradation in MoE reinforcement learning: when rollout and training engines differ in policy version, numerics, or execution, the same response can be routed to different experts during training, producing router drift and changing expert-level credit assignment.","importance":"The work is directly relevant to MoE post-training and the pretrain–posttrain interface. Token-level importance corrections only compare output probabilities and do not expose changes in expert assignment. Routing replay methods such as R3 enforce the rollout path, but they also suppress gradients to experts that the current training router would have selected. RDC is useful because it treats routing disagreement as a staleness signal and removes only high-drift responses, while preserving native routing for the retained data.","technical_approach":"Router Drift Control evaluates each sampled response under teacher forcing with both the rollout route and the current training route. For every MoE layer, it builds expert-usage distributions and aggregates their disagreement into a response-level router drift score. During policy optimization, the method rejects a small fraction of responses with the largest drift. The remaining responses are trained with the current model’s own routing decisions. This differs from R3, which reuses rollout expert selections and therefore changes gradient attribution. RDC is complementary to truncated importance sampling: TIS corrects token-probability mismatch, while RDC targets routing-path mismatch.","experiments":"The evaluation covers both synchronous and fully asynchronous RL, with larger gains as policy staleness increases. In asynchronous training, RDC combined with TIS improves the average peak validation accuracy on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 by 5.5 points over TIS alone and by 4.5 points over R3, while keeping training dynamics stable. The main ablation message is that routing disagreement is concentrated in a subset of responses; filtering those responses is less biased than replaying rollout routes and captures an MoE-specific off-policy failure mode that probability ratios miss.","reading_guide":"A 25–35 minute skim is enough. Start with the introduction and Figures 1–2 to understand the three sources of off-policy mismatch and why MoE routing amplifies them. In the method section, focus on the definition of the drift score, layer-wise expert-usage aggregation, the filtering rule, and how RDC composes with TIS. In the experiments, prioritize the asynchronous RL setting, AIME 2024/2025 results, and the comparisons against TIS and R3. Existing RL systems commonly rely on asynchronous execution to overlap rollout generation with policy optimization for enhanced efficiency. However, this approach faces inherent limitations: mild policy staleness offers only limited training efficiency gains, whereas aggressive asynchrony can degrade model quality and","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T10:21:41Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21640.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 LLM RL post-training 里的一个老问题，但切得更深：rollout 生成远比策略更新贵，而且多轮 agent 轨迹长度、工具调用时延、样本难度都高度不均，导致同步训练里 trainer 经常等数据，异步训练里又会因为 stale policy 过大而掉质量。已有异步系统主要是在“生成和训练并行”这一层做文章，仍然保留了批次级的数据依赖，trainer 还是会因为 fresh rollout 不够而停顿。ReFlow 要解决的是：能不能在不明显破坏 RL 稳定性的前提下，把 rollout 需求进一步降下来，把训练和生成真正解耦，并且让资源分配随 workload 动态变化。","importance":"对预训练研究员来说，这不是 pretrain 方法论文，但它和 pretrain↔posttrain 接口很相关。原因有两点。第一，当前大模型能力提升越来越依赖 RL 阶段，尤其是 reasoning 和 agentic setting；如果 RL 的单位算力产出太低，前面预训练得到的 base model 很难被充分榨干。第二，这篇工作的核心启发不是单纯系统工程，而是 system–algorithm co-design：它把 experience replay 从传统 RL 里重新带回 LLM post-training，并把“少做昂贵 rollout、多复用近期样本”作为一等公民来设计执行框架。这个方向的价值在于，它提示了一个更一般的问题：当 post-training 的数据生成成本远高于梯度更新成本时，训练目标、off-policy 修正、样本复用率、资源编排应该一起设计，而不是分开看。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 replay-driven decoupling。直观讲，ReFlow 不再把 trainer 严格绑在“等一批新 rollout 再更新”这个节奏上，而是维护一个可复用的近期经验池，让 trainer 可以持续从 replay buffer 中取样训练，同时 rollout workers 异步补充新轨迹。这样做的关键不是简单把 off-policy RL 套进来，而是控制 policy mismatch，避免旧样本把训练带偏。\n\n从论文给出的动机看，作者的设计有三层。第一层是执行层完全解耦：rollout 和 training 在资源上分离，trainer 不再受 batch-ready fresh data 的硬约束，减少长尾生成造成的 GPU bubble。第二层是算法层引入近期经验重放，并配合 staleness-aware 的样本使用策略与修正机制，让 replay 样本主要来自“足够近”的行为策略，从而在效率和稳定性之间取平衡。文中还给出理论分析，说明适度 replay 能降低梯度方差，这一点很重要，因为 LLM RL 的 reward 噪声和序列长度方差都很大，方差下降直接关系到 update 的可用性。第三层是运行时自适应：由于 rollout/training 的相对瓶颈会随训练阶段、任务类型、模型大小、硬件平台变化，ReFlow 会动态调整资源分配和生成并发，而不是固定 rollout GPU 数、trainer GPU 数和并发度。\n\n和常见异步 PPO/actor-learner 系统相比，区别主要有三点。其一，已有方法通常只接受轻度 stale on-policy 数据，目标是隐藏一部分生成延迟；ReFlow 更进一步，把 replay 作为降低 rollout 需求的主要手段。其二，已有系统常把资源配置视为静态超参；ReFlow 把 workload adaptation 放进系统设计。其三，作者强调的是“近期样本复用”而不是大跨度 off-policy 学习，这说明它仍然服务于 LLM RL 里对稳定性的高要求，而不是追求传统离策略算法那种极端样本效率。","experiments":"从引言能确认的实验重点有三类。第一类是端到端训练效率：作者要证明 fully decoupled replay 机制能减少 trainer 等待 fresh rollout 的时间，并提升整体 GPU 利用率，尤其在 response length 和 tool latency 长尾明显的多轮 agent 任务上收益更大。第二类是质量—效率权衡：与同步 RL、轻度异步 RL、以及不带 replay 或 replay 受限的变体相比，ReFlow 需要展示在相近质量下更少 rollout、更短 wall-clock，或者在相近时间下更高 reward。第三类是稳定性与消融：包括 replay ratio / buffer freshness / staleness 控制 / 动态资源调度是否必要，以及理论上声称的梯度方差降低是否在实证上成立。\n\n可惜给到的全文片段没有覆盖结果表和具体数字，所以没法可靠填写参数量、任务规模、加速倍数、reward 提升幅度等硬指标。能确定的是，作者把 multi-turn agentic RL 作为主要受益场景，因为这类任务 rollout 成本高、轨迹长、环境交互不规则，最能放大 decoupling 和 replay 的价值。阅读时应重点核对：1）replay 使用了多旧的样本；2）是否做 importance weighting 或 clipping；3）不同 staleness 区间对最终性能的影响；4）动态调度带来的收益是否独立于 replay 本身。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但建议把方法和实验细节看清。先看 Introduction wall-clock、GPU utilization、sample efficiency、以及多轮 agent 任务上的 ablation。预计 30–45 分钟可以抓住主线；如果你在做 RLHF/RLAIF 系统，值得再花时间看 appendix 里的调度策略和工程实现。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical bottleneck in LLM RL post-training: rollout generation is much more expensive than policy optimization, and the cost is highly variable across prompts, response lengths, and multi-turn tool interactions. Synchronous pipelines waste trainer time waiting for fresh batches, while asynchronous pipelines improve utilization only up to the point where policy staleness starts hurting learning. ReFlow asks a sharper question: can we reduce the demand for expensive fresh rollouts, decouple generation from optimization more aggressively, and still keep RL stable enough for LLM post-training?","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it matters for foundation-model research because post-training is now a major source of capability gains, especially for reasoning and agentic behavior. If RL remains rollout-bound, the effective value extracted from a pretrained base model is capped by systems inefficiency. The more interesting contribution is the joint systems-and-algorithm framing: instead of treating experience replay as something mostly irrelevant to modern LLM RL, the paper reintroduces it as a first-class mechanism for reducing rollout demand. That is useful beyond this specific system. It suggests a broader design principle for post-training: when data generation is much more expensive than gradient updates, sample reuse, off-policy correction, scheduling, and resource allocation should be designed together.","technical_approach":"$45","experiments":"$46","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it deserves a careful pass over the method and experiments. However, existing memory methods often optimize retrieval and memory construction separately, without a shared reward space for evaluating search, writing, and stopping decisions. To address this, we propose All-in-One Memory (AiO-Mem), which organizes these operations as transitions over a shared evidence state and directly evalua","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T03:02:24Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21147.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 memory agent 里一个长期存在但常被拆开做的问题：检索、写入、过滤、停止这些 memory 操作彼此强耦合，但现有方法通常把 retrieval policy 和 memory construction policy 分开训练，奖励也不在同一个语义空间里。结果是，系统可能拿到还可以的最终答案，却无法判断中间每一步 search / add / skip / stop 到底是不是语义上合理、是否真的为后续推理提供了必要证据。AiO-Mem 想解决的不是单点检索精度，而是把整个 memory interaction 过程统一成一个 evidence-state MDP，并给这个过程提供可学习的、逐步的过程奖励。","importance":"对基座研究的直接意义不在于它提出了一个新的预训练目标，而在于它把“外部记忆如何与语言模型协同”从模块拼接问题推进到了统一策略学习问题。这个方向和长上下文、RAG、persistent memory、tool-use agent 都有接口。更具体地说，它提供了两个有启发的点。第一，memory 不是静态索引，而是一个会被策略持续改写的工作状态；一旦这样看，训练目标就不该只盯 final answer。第二，过程奖励如果能落在 source-grounded evidence state 上，就有机会把 retrieval quality、compression faithfulness、redundancy control、stopping sufficiency 放进同一个优化框架。对做 pretrain/post-train 接口的人，这篇文章的价值在于它展示了如何把 rule signal、RM signal、trajectory preference 组合成一个较密的训练信号，而不是只靠 outcome RL。","technical_approach":"方法由四部分组成。第一步是 Traceable Multi-View Event Bank。作者先把异构历史——多轮对话、长文档、事实记录等——整理成可追溯的 event units，并保留 source trace。这里的关键不是简单切 chunk，而是把不同粒度的原始历史编译成统一的“事件视图”，让后续 retrieval 和 memory writing 都在同一底座上操作。第二步是 Evidence-State Transition Modeling。memory policy actor 在一个有限步长的 MDP 中行动，动作被组织成两阶段：先 decide，是继续 search 还是 stop；如果 search，再进入 extract，对检索到的 candidate 决定 add 还是 skip。这样做的好处是把停止决策显式化，也把“检索到但不写入”作为一等动作，而不是隐含在阈值里。第三步是 Unified Reward Modeling。作者训练一个 trajectory-conditioned reward model，不只看最终答案，而是对轨迹中的每个 memory action 打分。训练信号来自 evidence-grounded SFT、trajectory-level DPO，以及规则派生监督和 reasoning model 分数。这里的设计重点是 reward model 的输入包含当前 evidence state 和动作上下文，因此它评估的是动作在当前状态下是否合适，而不是静态地评估某段文本好不好。第四步是 Process-Aligned DAPO。作者在 DAPO 基础上做了 action-balanced trajectory reward aggregation，避免某一类高频动作主导总奖励，然后用 PA-DAPO 优化 memory policy actor。和已有 retrieval-only 或 write-only RL 方法相比，AiO-Mem 的区别不在于动作集合更大，而在于它把这些动作放进了共享状态空间和共享奖励空间里。","experiments":"从给出的全文片段看，实验结论是 AiO-Mem 在长上下文 memory benchmark 上同时提升 answer accuracy 和 reasoning faithfulness，并且优于静态 memory 系统以及分别做 RL 的 memory agent。作者特别强调了两类结果。第一类是任务结果：在多种长历史 QA 场景中，统一策略优于把 search 与 write 分开优化的系统。第二类是过程质量：reward model 能区分语义上更好的 memory trajectory，即便最终答案相同，AiO-Mem 也更少出现冗余写入、无效继续搜索、过早停止或证据不忠实压缩。消融上，值得注意的点大概率包括：去掉 unified reward model 后，策略会退化成 outcome-driven；去掉 action-balanced aggregation 后，高频动作会挤压 stop / add 等关键低频动作的学习；去掉 traceable event bank 后，reward grounding 和 memory faithfulness 会下降。当前提供的正文片段没有完整表格和具体数字，因此无法可靠给出参数量、训练 token、绝对提升幅度等硬指标；如果要精读，建议直接核对实验章节中的主表、faithfulness 指标定义和 reward model 消融表。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不建议把时间花在所有实现细节上。先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1/2，抓住作者要解决的是“分裂的 memory objective”而不是普通 RAG。然后重点看 Section 3.3 和 3.4：前者定义 shared evidence state 与 decide/extract 两阶段动作，后者解释 reward model 如何从规则信号、DPO 和 reasoning score 学到逐步过程奖励。实验部分建议优先看三类内容：主结果表、trajectory quality 或 faithfulness 分析、以及 reward / policy 的消融。预计 25–35 分钟能读完关键内容。真正值得深挖的是两个问题：一，reward model 是否学到了可迁移的 memory semantics，还是只拟合了特定 benchmark 的轨迹偏好；二，统一动作空间是否会带来 credit assignment 变难，尤其在长轨迹和稀疏 stop 决策上。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a coordination problem in memory agents: retrieval, writing, filtering, and stopping are tightly coupled, yet prior work usually optimizes retrieval and memory construction as separate modules with partially disconnected objectives. That setup makes final-answer reward a poor proxy for the semantic quality of intermediate memory actions. Two trajectories can reach the same answer while differing substantially in evidence relevance, faithfulness of memory writing, redundancy control, and stopping sufficiency. AiO-Mem reframes the whole memory interaction loop as a single evidence-state MDP and tries to learn process-level supervision over the full trajectory.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is indirect but real. The paper is less about a new pretraining objective and more about how external memory should be trained as part of a language-model system. That matters for long-context modeling, persistent memory, RAG, and tool-using agents. The main contribution is conceptual: memory is treated as a dynamically edited working state rather than a static retrieval backend, so optimization should not rely on final-answer reward alone. The second useful idea is methodological: rule signals, reward modeling, and trajectory preferences are combined into a denser supervision channel for memory behavior. For researchers thinking about the pretrain–post-train interface, this is a concrete example of process supervision applied to evidence manipulation rather than answer generation.","technical_approach":"$47","experiments":"$48","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T03:24:42Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21167.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 agentic search / deep research 数据合成里的一个关键失真：很多合成流程会把问题做得更长、更绕、证据更多，但这些新增证据未必是求解所必需的，于是“标称难度”并不等于“实际推理难度”。如果训练数据的难度标签不可信，后续的 SFT、RL、课程学习和评测都会被污染。GRADS 的核心问题因此不是单纯生成更多 QA，而是生成难度可控、且这种难度能在求解时真实体现出来的数据。","importance":"对预训练本身的直接关系有限，它更偏 post-train 数据工程；但对基座研究仍有启发，尤其是数据质量与训练信号设计。它给出的重要判断是：数据复杂度不能靠表面结构近似，必须靠必要推理链来定义。作者用 evidence graph 把“需要几步推理”从模糊直觉变成了可操作对象，这对合成 reasoning data、tool-use data、search agent data 都有参考价值。另一个值得注意的点是，文章把 difficulty control、source grounding、answer verification 放在同一流水线里，而不是先生成再事后筛。对于做持续训练或中期训练的人，这种思路比单纯扩大合成数据量更稳，因为它直接约束了训练信号的真实性和可解释性。","technical_approach":"GRADS 的方法核心是 evidence graph。图中的节点是 source-grounded claims，边表示到达答案所必需的推理步骤，图深度用来表示题目的目标难度。作者据此构建一个 graph-regulated synthesis loop，而不是像常见方法那样通过增加实体、约束、干扰信息来“看起来更难”。整个流程包括 seed selection、evidence expansion、question-answer construction、verification 几个环节，但 difficulty control 不是由探索过程自然产生，而是由 evidence graph 的结构显式约束。这个设计把 exploration 和 difficulty control 解耦：agent 可以在大语料里自由搜索候选证据，但最终被接受的样本必须满足目标图深度和必要性要求。为了解决真实大规模语料难以穷举的问题，作者先对原始记录做清洗和聚类，并提供统一的本地与在线搜索工具。为了解决 LLM 合成答案不可靠的问题，每个答案都附带由 atomic claims 组成的 rubric，并且每条 claim 都要绑定 verbatim source spans，再由 verification subagents 逐项核验。和已有 graph sampling 或 task extension 方法相比，GRADS 的关键区别是它不把图当作素材来源，而是把图当作难度约束器；不把“证据更多”当作“推理更深”的替代品。","experiments":"实验覆盖三个异构领域：GitHub issue tracking、实验粒子物理、security。作者构建了对应 benchmark 和 post-training corpus，并与三类合成基线比较。最关键的结果有两点。第一，只有 GRADS 生成的数据在 requested difficulty 从 Easy 到 Hard 提升时，delivered reasoning depth 也单调增加；同时，不同能力层级的 solver 在这些数据上的得分会随难度单调下降。这说明它控制的是实际求解难度，而不是题面长度。第二，用 GRADS 轨迹对 4B 模型做 deep-search agents: many pipelines make questions look harder by adding more evidence, more entities, or more constraints, but the added material is often not necessary for solving the problem. As a result, nominal difficulty diverges from realized reasoning difficulty. That breaks both evaluation and training, because difficulty labels stop being trustworthy signals. GRADS is designed to synthesize QA data whose requested difficulty is actually reflected in the solver’s reasoning path.","importance":"This is more post-training data work than core pretraining, but it is still relevant to foundation-model research through the lens of data quality and supervision design. The paper makes a useful point: complexity should be defined by necessary inference structure, not by surface expansion. By introducing an evidence graph whose depth corresponds to required reasoning depth, it turns a vague notion of 'harder data' into something operational. That matters for synthetic reasoning data, search-agent training, curriculum design, and benchmark construction. Another practical contribution is that difficulty control, source grounding, and answer verification are built into one generation pipeline rather than treated as separate cleanup stages.","technical_approach":"$49","experiments":"$4a","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. We co","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T16:19:16Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22152.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这项工作用一个可控的清洁机器人 Frequently, researchers do not have full access to a teacher model and its internal representations, which limits the resulting performance. We propose a recursive offline distillation method combining two components: entropy-based sampling for selective distillation and answer mixing. Specifically, for three open models (3-4B), at each epoch, we sample reasoning chains","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-14T20:45:04Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_20914.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在无法获取教师模型内部表示的离线蒸馏场景下，如何高效地将大型语言模型（LLM）的推理能力蒸馏到资源受限的小模型中，同时解决数据选择和模型能力保持的问题。","importance":"提出了一种适用于资源受限环境的自动化递归离线蒸馏管道。通过引入基于熵增益的采样策略和答案混合机制，在相同数据预算下显著提升了小模型的推理能力，为低成本模型微调提供了一种有效范式。","technical_approach":"方法包含两个核心组件：1) 熵增益采样（Entropy Gain Sampling）：利用较大代理模型（70-72B）和学生模型（3-4B）在单 token 预测上的熵差（近似认知不确定性差异）作为启发式指标，在每个 epoch 动态加权采样合成的推理链数据，引导学生模型学习其知识盲区。2) 答案混合（Answer Mixing）：在训练数据中按比例（1024条推理链配256条单token答案）混入直接输出答案的样本，以保持模型非推理任务的能力，并意外地带来了显著的性能提升。整个过程在单张 80GB 显卡上通过 LoRA 递归进行。","experiments":"在 MMLU Pro 数据集上，使用 Qwen2.5 3B、Phi-4-Mini 等作为学生模型。结果表明，该方法在同等数据预算下，平均准确率达到 57.15 ± 1.01，显著优于朴素蒸馏基线的 51.61。消融实验显示，贪婪采样策略表现不稳定，而本文的动态加权采样和答案混合机制是性能提升的关键。数据混合策略仅增加了不到 0.01% 的训练 token，却带来了实质性收益。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 Section 3 的方法描述，特别是熵增益采样的动机（基于认知-偶然不确定性分解）和 Algorithm 1 的具体流程。Section 4 的实验结果（Table 1 和 Figure 2）展示了该方法相比基线的优势。预计阅读时间 10 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to efficiently distill reasoning capabilities from Large Language Models (LLMs) into smaller models in an offline setting where access to the teacher's internal representations is unavailable, while addressing data selection and capability retention.","importance":"Proposes an automated, recursive offline distillation pipeline suitable for resource-constrained environments. By introducing entropy gain-based sampling and answer mixing, it significantly improves the reasoning capabilities of small models under the same data budget, offering an effective paradigm for low-cost model fine-tuning.","technical_approach":"The method consists of two core components: 1) Entropy Gain Sampling: Uses the difference in single-token prediction entropy between a larger proxy model (70-72B) and the student model (3-4B) as a heuristic (approximating epistemic uncertainty difference). It dynamically samples synthetic reasoning chains weighted by this gain at each epoch, guiding the student to learn its knowledge gaps. 2) Answer Mixing: Mixes direct single-token answer samples into the training data (ratio of 1024 reasoning chains to 256 single-token answers) to preserve the model's non-reasoning capabilities, which surprisingly yields a substantial performance uplift. The entire process is executed recursively using LoRA on a single 80GB GPU.","experiments":"Evaluated on the MMLU Pro dataset using student models like Qwen2.5 3B and Phi-4-Mini. The proposed method achieves an average accuracy of 57.15 ± 1.01, significantly outperforming the naive distillation baseline of 51.61 under comparable data budgets. Ablation studies show that greedy sampling is unstable, while the dynamic weighted sampling and answer mixing are crucial for the performance gain. The data mixing strategy adds less than 0.01% training tokens but provides substantial benefits.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 3 for the method description, especially the motivation for entropy gain sampling (based on epistemic-aleatoric uncertainty decomposition) and the specific workflow in Algorithm 1. s","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T02:30:15Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21125.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文处理的是少步生成模型的黑盒奖励对齐问题。少步模型只用一次或少数几次函数评估生成样本，直接套用扩散模型里的 RL 微调容易破坏预训练分布；逐样本做 test-time noise search 又会把推理成本拉高，抵消少步生成的效率优势。","importance":"这篇工作的启发点在于把对齐对象从模型参数移到输入噪声分布。对预训练研究来说，它提供了一种较干净的后训练接口：冻结生成器，只学习一个噪声空间的策略分布，让奖励优化不直接扰动预训练模型的知识。这个思路对少步 diffusion、flow、离散语言生成以及蛋白结构生成都有参考意义，尤其适合奖励不可微、生成路径近似确定、模型参数不宜被重写的场景。","technical_approach":"方法叫 Noise Policy Optimization, NPO。给定冻结的少步生成器 gθ 和标量奖励 r(x)，目标是采样 reward-tilted output distribution，即在基模型分布上按 exp(r/α) 重新加权后的输出分布。论文利用确定性采样路径的性质：如果 x=gθ(z)，那么在输出空间做 reward tilting，可以等价转化为在输入噪声 z 空间做 reward tilting。NPO 因此训练一个 amortized flow policy，把标准高斯噪声变换到高奖励噪声区域。和直接微调生成器相比，它不改动 gθ；和 test-time optimization 相比，它把每个样本都要做的搜索摊销到训练阶段。另一个技术点是似然估计。传统 diffusion/flow RL 常用轨迹似然估计，需要沿生成轨迹累积条件概率。NPO 改用基于 endpoint noise samples 的 ELBO 式似然估计，减少对完整轨迹估计的依赖，提高奖励评估的样本效率。","experiments":"实验覆盖图像、语言和蛋白结构生成任务，包含 one-step 和 few-step generators，以及不同黑盒奖励函数。主要结论有三点。第一，直接生成器微调在少步 regime 下不稳定，要么奖励提升有限，要么出现 reward over-optimization，样本质量和多样性下降。第二，NPO 在奖励提升、样本质量保持和多样性之间给出更好的折中，因为生成器参数保持冻结。第三，相比逐样本 test-time noise optimization，NPO 达到相当或更高奖励，同时推理成本降低 16 到 549 倍。和基于轨迹似然的 noise-policy training 相比，NPO 用更少 reward likelihood 相对 trajectory likelihood 的 reward-evaluation efficiency。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies black-box reward alignment for one-step and few-step generative models. Direct RL fine-tuning does not transfer cleanly to this regime because small parameter updates can damage the pretrained generator. Per-sample test-time noise search can improve rewards, but it removes the main efficiency advantage of few-step generation.","importance":"The useful idea is to move alignment from parameter space to input-noise space. For pretraining and post-training research, this gives a lightweight interface to a pretrained generator: keep the generator fixed, and learn a policy over its initial noise distribution. This is relevant beyond images, especially for few-step diffusion or flow models, language generation with non-differentiable rewards, and structure generation where reward gradients are unavailable or unreliable.","technical_approach":"Noise Policy Optimization learns an amortized flow policy over the generator’s input noise. The target is the reward-tilted output distribution, obtained by reweighting the base generator distribution by an exponential reward term with KL regularization. When the sampling path is deterministic, sampling from this tilted output distribution can be reduced to sampling from a corresponding tilted input-noise distribution. NPO trains a flow to transport standard Gaussian noise toward that target noise distribution. This differs from direct RL because the pretrained generator is frozen, and differs from test-time noise optimization because the search is amortized during training. The paper also replaces conventional trajectory-based likelihood estimation with an ELBO-style endpoint likelihood estimator over sampled noise endpoints, which improves reward-sample efficiency in this setting.","experiments":"The evaluation spans image, language, and protein structure generation, with both one-step and few-step generators and several black-box reward functions. Direct generator fine-tuning either makes limited reward progress or over-optimizes the reward while degrading sample quality. NPO gives a better reward-quality-diversity trade-off by preserving the frozen generator. Compared with test-time noise optimization, it achieves comparable or higher rewards with 16 to 549 times lower inference cost. Compared with trajectory-based noise-policy training, it reaches similar reward levels with fewer reward evaluations.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Start with the introduction and Figure 1 to understand why direct fine-tuning and test-time search fail in different ways. The method section is worth reading for the flow policy and the endpoint ELBO likelihood estimator. targeted undesirable instruction following can be reduced through a lightweight weight edit, without gradient-based training, defensive system prompts, or inference-time activation steering.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model research is the intervention interface. The method stores a behavior change in the weights using only forward passes over paired prompts. This sits between prompting, activation steering, and full fine-tuning. It is useful for studying how instruction-following circuits and safety constraints can be separated, and it may serve as a low-cost safety patch when the target behavior is narrow and well specified.","technical_approach":"The method builds paired prompts for a target behavior: one prompt requests the undesirable behavior, and the matched prompt requests a desirable alternative while keeping the underlying content fixed. For each linear matrix in attention and MLP blocks, the method records input-channel activations under both prompt conditions. A weight receives a high score when its magnitude is large and its input channel is more active under the undesirable instruction than under the desirable one. The highest-scoring weights within each matrix are then rescaled. Embeddings and the output head are left unchanged. The procedure uses no gradients and no labeled model outputs. Its main distinction from prior activation-based weight-importance methods is the graded activation-contrast score, rather than selecting weights through set differences between separately ranked lists.","experiments":"The evaluation uses five instruction-tuned model families and two target behaviors. On instructed falsehood, the edited models reach 84.9% mean honest response rate on LIARS’ BENCH and 79.4% on AI Liar over three sampling seeds, outperforming the strongest alternative by 6.2 and 4.5 percentage points. The per-model gain is significant for three of five models and within noise for the other two. A seven-format prompt-transfer study shows better transfer to unseen single-turn formats than single-layer activation steering built from the same facts. For instructed sexism, the mean sexist-response rate drops from 57.9% in unedited models to 24.3% after weight editing. A fairness-oriented system instruction alone reduces it to 30.2%, and combining the instruction with the edit reduces it to 11.9%. The paper also reports checks on general capability, compliance with legitimate requests, transfer to other tasks, and a preliminary human evaluation of automated-grader agreement.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. However, despite alignment training, these models still exhibit dishonest behaviour, such as outputting claims that contradict some knowledge or are not supported by what is accessible in context. We introduce Continuous Adversarial Training for Consistent Honesty (CATCH), inspired by a method originally developed to robustly refuse harmful queries. CATCH trains the model towards an honest answer and away","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T17:43:47Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2610_22292.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.CY"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个比“事实性”更窄、但对对齐很关键的问题：模型在明知或可知真实信息的情况下，仍然会输出与其知识或上下文相矛盾的内容。作者把这种 dishonest behavior 分成三类来源：系统提示明确施压要求说谎、在多智能体博弈中为了更高回报而撒谎、以及通过训练学会隐瞒真相。核心问题不是单一场景下把模型拉回诚实，而是能否用一个统一训练干预，让诚实行为在不同诱因下都能迁移和保持，同时尽量不伤一般能力。","importance":"对预训练研究员来说，这篇更偏 post-training/robust alignment，而不是基座训练本身。但它有两个值得注意的点。第一，它把 honesty 当成一种可迁移的行为特征来训练，而不是做任务特定的 prompt patch 或大规模 SFT，这对理解“模型行为能否以少量目标样本被稳定塑形”有参考价值。第二，它延续了 adversarial training 在线性空间附近寻找最坏扰动的思路，说明很多对齐属性也许可以被看成局部鲁棒性问题，而不是纯监督覆盖问题。这个视角对 reward hacking、deception、以及 RL 后训练中的目标错配都有启发。量级上，它更像一个方法学信号，而不是会直接改变预训练 recipe 的工作。","technical_approach":"方法叫 CATCH，来自对 CAT（Continuous Adversarial Training）的改造。训练样本不是普通 instruction-response，而是为每个带有“说谎压力”的 prompt x 构造一对目标 continuation：y+ 是与模型已有知识或上下文一致的诚实回答，y- 是顺从压力、陈述相反命题的 dishonest 回答。训练目标同时做两件事：把模型朝 y+ 拉近，把模型从 y- 推远。\n\n难点在于，诚实训练不像安全拒答那样可以用统一的 toward target。拒答任务里，几乎所有 harmful prompt 都可以朝一个通用 refusal 模板收缩；但 honesty 的 truth/lie 都依赖具体命题和上下文，所以每个样本都要单独构造正负目标。这也是为什么作者没有直接用普通 SFT：SFT 只能在见过的 prompt 模式上拟合目标分布，面对新的施压方式、博弈环境或 concealment setting，泛化往往不够。\n\nCATCH 的关键是把“最坏情况下会诱发 y- 的 prompt 扰动”放到 embedding 空间里近似搜索。具体做法是：对输入 token embedding 加有界扰动 δ，在一个约束集合内用 K 步 projected signed gradient ascent 最大化 log pθ(y-|x+δ)，得到局部最坏扰动；然后在外层优化中，让模型在这些 adversarially perturbed prompts 上降低 y- 的概率、提高 y+ 的概率。这样训练出来的不是对固定 wording 的诚实，而是对一小片 prompt 邻域的诚实鲁棒性。\n\n论文还强调 capability preservation。作者不是只拿 honesty 数据做训练，而是混入 utility dataset，防止模型因为强行压制某类 continuation 而整体退化。更有意思的是，他们研究了 utility dataset 的选择，发现不同 utility 数据对能力保持影响很大，并提出了一个更合适的选择策略。和 honesty steering 相比，CATCH 的优点是训练后行为更稳定，不依赖推理时额外干预；和大规模 honesty SFT 相比，它用的数据少得多，靠的是 adversarial objective 带来的局部泛化。","experiments":"从正文可确认的结果有几项。第一，训练数据规模很小：只用了大约 200 个 target，来源于 self-reporting scenarios。第二，作者在两个模型上验证：Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 和 Qwen3.6-27B。第三，泛化评测覆盖三种不同 dishonesty 来源：MASK 上的 instructed dishonesty、Among Us 上由博弈激励诱发的 dishonesty、以及 AuditBench 上通过训练学会 conceal truth 的情形。作者声称 CATCH 在这些设置下都能迁移，说明它学到的不是单一模板。\n\n和基线比较上，文中明确说它优于所有 baseline，并且在 MASK 上与当时最强的 honesty steering 方法相当；同时，它超过了一个 honesty-SFT 实现，而后者用了大约 50 倍更多的 honesty 数据。这一点是论文最有信息量的实验结论：少量目标样本 + adversarial training，可能比大规模直接监督更能学到可迁移的 honesty behavior。\n\n另一个值得看的实验是 utility dataset 的消融。作者发现 utility 数据的构成会显著影响 general capability preservation，并给出一种新的选择方式，能力保持甚至优于已有 honesty steering。给到的全文片段没有表格数字，所以无法可靠写出具体分数差值、下降幅度或各 benchmark 的绝对指标。阅读时应重点核对：1）honesty 提升是否伴随 refusal 增多；2）能力保持是看哪些通用 benchmark；3）不同 attack step、扰动半径 ε、toward/away loss 权重对结果的影响；4）是否存在只对 self-reporting 类 prompt 有利的偏置。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读到中等深度。先看 Introduction，作者对 dishonesty 的定义比很多“事实性”论文更严格，区分了 knowledge/context contradiction 与一般幻觉。然后看 Methods，重点理解为什么 honesty 不能像 refusal training 那样用统一 target，以及 embedding-space adversarial search 具体怎么做。若时间有限，公式只要抓住 inner maximization 和 outer minimization 的关系即可。实验部分优先看三套 benchmark：MASK、Among Us、AuditBench，再看与 honesty steering、honesty-SFT 的对比，以及 utility dataset 消融。预计 25–35 分钟能读完主线；如果你关心 deception、reward hacking 或 robust alignment，可以再看 appendix 里的 target construction 和 capability eval 细节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a narrower but more consequential alignment problem than generic factuality: models sometimes produce statements that contradict what they know or what is available in context, even when the truth is accessible. The authors focus on three sources of such dishonesty: explicit pressure to lie in the prompt, strategic incentives to lie in a multi-agent game, and dishonesty induced by prior training to conceal the truth. The central question is whether a single training intervention can make honesty transfer across these different failure modes while preserving general capability.","importance":"For a pretraining-oriented reader, this is mainly a post-training robustness paper, not a base-model training paper. Still, it is relevant for two reasons. First, it treats honesty as a transferable behavioral property rather than a task-specific prompt trick or a large supervised corpus problem. That is useful for thinking about how small amounts of targeted data can reshape model behavior when the objective is chosen well. Second, it frames honesty as a robustness problem and applies adversarial training in embedding space, suggesting that some alignment properties may be better handled through local worst-case training than through broader supervised coverage alone. That perspective connects to deception, reward hacking, and objective misspecification in RL post-training.","technical_approach":"$4e","experiments":"$4f","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it is worth reading beyond the abstract. Start with the introduction, because the paper defines dishonesty more carefully than many factuality papers and separates contradiction-to-knowledge/context from generic hallucination. Then read the method section with one question in mind: why can’t honesty be trained like refusal? Once that is clear, the embedding-space adversarial search becomes much easier to interpret. If time is limited, you only need the high-level relation between the inner maximization and the outer minimization; the exact optimization details are secondary. time, from few examples, with minimal human priors) and argues that today’s LLM practice largely industrializes a closed-world loop. Developmental studies usually answer this by tracking when models start producing the correct token. But that prediction passes through the unembedding matrix, or readout, which is itself still being learned during pretraining. We show that a model's hidden states can encode task information before its own readout expresses it, so timelines read from outputs conflate what the model encodes with what its readout expresses. To track the readout dire","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-14T23:45:26Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21025.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是语言模型“能力何时出现”的测量混淆。常见做法用 checkpoint 的输出正确率、surprisal 或 logit lens 来给能力定年，但这些指标都经过 unembedding/readout 矩阵。readout 也在训练中变化，所以输出变化可能来自隐藏状态已经编码了信息，也可能来自 readout 才学会把这些信息投到正确 token 上。论文把这两件事拆开：信息何时可从 hidden state 线性读出，和模型自己的 readout 何时能原生表达出来。","importance":"对预训练研究的影响在于，它提醒早期训练动态评估不能只看 token 输出。很多 developmental study 会在训练前 1% 左右寻找 induction head、语法能力、翻译行为等转折点，但如果 readout 还在快速重组，这些转折点可能反映的是读出层成熟，而不是内部表示刚刚形成。贡献量级不是一个新训练 recipe，而是一个测量框架：把 hidden-state availability 和 native expression 分开，对解释 checkpoint eval、logit lens、early capability emergence、训练配方早期比较都有直接影响。对预训练实践也有一个具体启发：只改变 readout 学习率就能改变早期能力显现时间，但最终 loss 变化很小，说明早期 benchmark 差异可能包含 readout 发展速度这一隐变量。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 parameter-trajectory crosscoding。它不是在激活上训练 sparse autoencoder，而是在所有 checkpoint 的 unembedding/readout 权重行上训练一个共享稀疏字典。每个 token 的 readout row 在每个 checkpoint 都被视为一条参数轨迹；模型学习一组 feature，每个 token 对 feature 有共享 code，每个 checkpoint 下 feature 有对应的 decoder direction 和 gate。这样同一个 feature 可以跨 checkpoint 被追踪，避免了逐 checkpoint 训练 SAE 后再做 feature matching 的不稳定问题。论文还对每个 checkpoint 的 readout rows 做中心化和尺度归一化，使 feature norm 更接近结构变化，而不是整体范数增长。另一条分析线是 readout swap：固定某个 checkpoint 的 hidden state，用不同 checkpoint 的 readout 去解码。logit 的变化被拆成 hidden state 变化、readout 变化以及二者交互，而不是把输出正确率当作单一学习信号。以 subject-verb agreement 为例，如果 hidden state 中 subject number 已可被 probe 读出，但当前 readout 仍偏向错误动词，就说明能力处于 available but not natively expressed 的阶段。","experiments":"主要实验集中在 Pythia 系列 checkpoint。论文发现 readout 的关键重组发生在极早期、很窄的窗口：约训练进度的 0.4% 到 1.1%。在这个阶段，crosscoder feature 的方向和范数快速变化，WordNet 语义类别开始能从 readout rows 中被解码出来，说明词表输出空间在早期形成了可解释的类别结构。Pythia-1B 和 Pythia-6.9B 上的 subject number 实验显示，hidden states 先于模型自己的 readout 编码相关语法信息：用 probe 或替换为后期 readout 能更早读出正确 number，但用同 checkpoint 自身 readout 还不能稳定表达为正确 token。训练干预部分在小规模 controlled pretraining run 中只提高 readout 的学习率，结果是 readout 更早成熟，native expression 更早追上 availability，同时最终 loss 几乎不受影响。这一消融支持了论文的核心因果说法：早期能力曲线的一部分来自读出层学习速度，而不是主体网络表示学习速度。","reading_guide":"建议精读 Introduction 中 Figure 1 和 logit 分解部分，那里定义了为什么输出定年会混合 hidden state 与 readout 两个来源。Section 2.1 是方法核心，重点看 parameter-trajectory crosscoding 的参数化方式、共享 code 与 checkpoint-specific decoder 的设计，以及为什么它比逐 checkpoint SAE 更适合追踪 readout 轨迹。Section 2.3 值得细看，尤其是 readout swap 与 probing 如何对应 availability/expression 两个概念。实验部分优先看 Section 4.1、4.2、4.3、4.5：分别对应 readout 早期重组、WordNet 类别可解码性、subject number 的表示-表达时间差、以及只调 readout 学习率的干预。值得深挖的问题有三个：第一，readout 的早期重组是否会影响 embedding tying 下输入 embedding 的组织；第二，其他架构或 tokenizer 下 0.4–1.1% 的窗口是否稳定；第三，训练配方比较中是否应单独报告 readout maturation 指标。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a measurement confound in dating when a language model acquires a capability. Output-based timelines pass through the unembedding matrix, and that readout is itself being learned during pretraining. A checkpoint can already encode task-relevant information in its hidden states while its own readout still fails to express that information as the correct token. The paper separates two notions: when information is linearly available in the hidden state, and when it is natively expressed by the model’s current readout.","importance":"The main value is methodological. Early-training analyses often date capabilities from logits, surprisal, checkpoint accuracy, or logit-lens projections. This paper shows that such dates can be shifted by the maturation speed of the readout. That matters for studies of early training transitions, checkpoint comparisons, and recipe comparisons made in the first percent of training. The training intervention is especially relevant for pretraining work: increasing only the readout learning rate makes native expression appear earlier with little effect on final loss. Early benchmark gains can therefore reflect faster readout development rather than earlier formation of the underlying representation.","technical_approach":"$51","experiments":"The empirical analysis uses Pythia checkpoints, with detailed results on Pythia-1B and Pythia-6.9B. The readout undergoes a narrow early reorganization window, around 0.4% to 1.1% through training. During this period, crosscoder features change rapidly and WordNet categories become decodable from readout rows, indicating that the vocabulary output space is acquiring semantic structure. In subject-verb agreement experiments, hidden states encode subject number before the model’s own readout can reliably express it as the correct verb token. Using later readouts to decode earlier hidden states reveals information that the checkpoint’s native readout misses. In controlled small-scale pretraining runs, raising only the readout learning rate moves readout development earlier and narrows the gap between availability and expression, while final loss changes little.","reading_guide":"Read Figure 1 and the logit decomposition first; they make the confound concrete. Section 2.3 is important for understanding the availability versus expression distinction and the readout-swap diagnostic. The most useful empirical sections are 4.1, 4.2, 4.3, and 4.5: early readout reorganization, WordNet decodability, subject-number availability before expression, and the readout-learning-rate intervention. The main result is that longer reasoning often pushes models toward literal logical readings rather than human-like pragmatic enrichment.","importance":"The work is relevant to foundation-model research because it exposes a concrete failure mode of inference-time deliberation and reasoning-oriented post-training. Extra compute is not a uniformly beneficial knob: it can change the model’s interpretation policy. The result also cautions against treating chain-of-thought traces as a stable belief trajectory, since models may entertain an enriched interpretation early and cancel it later.","technical_approach":"The study uses a fixed-weight dose-response setup. The model is held constant while the thinking mode, token budget, or reasoning effort is varied. Items are drawn from pragmatic inference datasets covering logical words, lexical scales, presuppositions, and context-dependent implicatures. For logical-word cases, the output is scored as pragmatic or literal; for context-driven cases, standard accuracy is used. A second diagnostic cuts the model’s own reasoning trace at successive token prefixes and forces an answer from each prefix. This prefix intervention measures how the endorsed reading changes during the trace, rather than only at the final answer. The protocol also includes shuffled options, committed-answer parsing, label-free likelihood readouts, and signal-detection analysis to control for response bias.","experiments":"The experiments cover 40 open reasoning models and 7 closed models, including Qwen3 variants, gpt-oss effort settings, and GPT-5. Extended reasoning reduces pragmatic readings for “some” and “or” in 30 of 40 open models and in GPT-5. A label-free three-way choice setup shows the same pattern in 21 of 24 tested models. The effect is not a blanket degradation: context-dependent inferences such as indirect answers tend to improve with thinking. Trace-prefix curves show a characteristic pattern where early prefixes often support the pragmatic reading, while the completed trace switches to the literal reading. For two 8B models, extracting the answer after the first 128 reasoning tokens improves scalar felicity accuracy without substantially harming literal cases.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Although previous work has uncovered substantial structure in the intermediate representations that networks use, little progress has been made to understand how the parameters and nonlinearities of networks perform computations on those representations. In this work, we present a method that brings us closer to this understanding by decomposing a language model'","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-14T18:06:00Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_20748.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作关注语言模型参数层面的机制解释：如何把模型权重分解成更小的参数子组件，使每个子组件对应较局部的计算，并且每个输入只需要少量子组件就能重构模型行为。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在于，它尝试把可解释性从 activation space 推到 parameter space。activation-based decomposition 可以解释中间表征，但常用的替代函数和原模型的参数、非线性并不一致，因此难以指导可预测的参数编辑，也难以判断机制在分布变化下是否稳定。VPD 如果能在更大模型上保持 faithful decomposition，可能帮助分析预训练中出现的模块化能力、参数复用、特征叠加，以及训练后如何做更精确的模型编辑。","technical_approach":"论文提出 adVersarial Parameter Decomposition，即 VPD。它延续 Stochastic Parameter Decomposition 的目标：将原模型参数表示为多个可加的 parameter components，并希望在单个样本上只激活少量必要组件；非必要组件被移除时，模型输出仍应被良好重构。关键改动是使用更强的 ablatability 标准，不只在随机 ablation 下保持 reconstruction，而是在 adversarially chosen ablations 下也要保持行为一致。这样可以减少一种常见问题：分解看似稀疏，但只有在温和扰动下有效，一旦选择更刁钻的删除组合就失真。相比 activation-based transcoders 或 mixture-of-linear-transforms，VPD 直接分解模型实际参数，因而更接近执行计算的对象，也更容易跨 attention、MLP 或其他架构使用。","experiments":"论文将 VPD 扩展到一个完整小型语言模型：约 67M 参数、4 层、在 The Pile 上训练。作者报告分解得到的参数子组件具有较好的可解释性：一方面可以通过触发这些组件的数据样本理解其语义或行为模式，另一方面可以分析组件之间如何组合产生特定模型行为。文中还强调了 adversarial ablation 下的输出重构损失，这是相对 SPD 更关键的 faithfulness 检验。由于给出的正文片段未包含完整表格和所有数值结果，可靠可摘取的具体数字主要是模型规模、层数和训练语料来源。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 30 到 45 分钟。先看 introduction 中对 activation-based decomposition 局限的论证，再看 VPD 的 objective 和 adversarial ablation 设计。实验部分优先看 细节上停留太久。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how to interpret language models at the level of parameters: decompose the weights into smaller components, make each component implement a limited part of the learned algorithm, and use only a small subset of components on any given input.","importance":"The contribution is relevant to pretraining research through mechanistic analysis rather than training recipes. Activation-based decompositions have explained many intermediate representations, but their surrogate functions do not match the actual parameters and nonlinearities that implement the model. That mismatch makes parameter editing and out-of-distribution mechanism analysis difficult. A faithful parameter-level decomposition could help study modularity, superposition, parameter reuse, and the stability of learned algorithms during and after pretraining.","technical_approach":"The method is adVersarial Parameter Decomposition, or VPD. It builds on Stochastic Parameter Decomposition by representing model weights as a sum of parameter components and encouraging sparse component usage per datapoint. Components that are unnecessary for a datapoint should be removable without increasing output reconstruction error. The main change is a stronger ablatability criterion: reconstruction must hold not only under stochastic ablations but also under adversarially selected ablations. This is meant to prevent decompositions that look sparse and faithful under easy perturbations but fail under harder component-removal patterns. Compared with activation-based transcoders or mixtures of linear transforms, VPD decomposes the actual parameters used by the model, which makes the account closer to the implemented computation and less tied to a particular architectural block.","experiments":"The paper applies VPD to a full small language model with 67M parameters and four layers, trained on The Pile. The reported decompositions produce interpretable parameter components, both through the dataset examples that activate them and through analyses of how components interact to generate specific behaviors. The paper also emphasizes output reconstruction under adversarial ablations as a key faithfulness metric relative to earlier SPD-style methods. The provided text does not include the full quantitative tables, so the reliably extractable concrete details are the 67M-parameter scale, four-layer architecture, and The Pile training source.","reading_guide":"A 30–45 minute skim is appropriate. Start with the argument against activation-only decompositions, then read the VPD objective and the adversarial ablation setup. In the experiments, focus on reconstruction loss, sparsity, robustness to adversarial ablation, and the qualitative examples of interpretable parameter components. For pretraining researchers, the most useful parts are the 67M Pile LM decomposition and the component-interaction case studies; toy examples can be read only as motivation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"机制可解释性（MI）领域的新进展，通过对抗性消融强制参数分解的稀疏性和独立性，对理解模型内部机制和潜在的模型剪枝/编辑有启发。","en":"A new advancement in mechanistic interpretability (MI) that enforces sparsity and independence in parameter decomposition via adversarial ablation; inspiring for understanding internal mechanisms and potential model pruning/editing."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Lucius Bushnaq","Dan Braun","Oliver Clive-Griffin","Bart Bussmann","Nathan Hu","Michael Ivanitskiy","Linda Linsefors","Lee Sharkey"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Mechanistic Interpretability","Parameter Decomposition","Adversarial Ablation","Sparsity"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.20748","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.20748","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.21176","title":"Language Models Share a Readout Bottleneck in Working Memory","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) reproduce a human working-memory signature on the N-back task: performance declines as memory load N increases, and errors concentrate on lures, items that repeat the current stimulus at a neighboring but incorrect serial position. We ask whether this shared behavioral signature reflects a shared mechanism. The literature offers two candidate accounts. On a precision account, softmax attention cannot concentrate its weight on the item N positions back, so adjacent it","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T03:32:37Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21176.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL","q-bio.NC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“LLM 在 N-back 上为什么会犯错”这么泛的问题，而是更具体的一点：LLM 与人类都表现出随记忆负载 N 增大而性能下降、并且容易被邻近位置 lure 干扰，这种相似行为到底对应相似机制，还是只是表面现象。作者把候选机制收敛到两个可检验假说：一是 precision account，认为错误来自注意力定位不准，softmax 无法把权重尖锐地压到恰好 N 步之前的位置，于是相邻位置分走权重；二是 interference account，认为模型其实找到了正确位置，但目标内容在后续读出阶段被其他近期项目的内容污染。论文的核心任务，是在预训练 LLM 和可控小模型里把这两种机制真正区分开，并给出因果证据，而不是只做相关性分析。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要有三层。第一，它提醒人不要把“上下文都还在输入里”误解成“模型没有工作记忆瓶颈”。即便没有显式遗忘，瓶颈仍可能出现在 readout，也就是从已检索内容形成最终决策的阶段。第二，它把一个常见但容易被滥用的解释——把错误归因于注意力不够尖锐——压缩到了更窄的适用范围：单层小 transformer 里这个解释成立，但在多层预训练 LLM 里，主导瓶颈更像内容干扰而不是位置选择失败。第三，这对长上下文建模、检索式计算、memory 机制设计都有启发：如果问题主要在读出混叠，那么单纯改 attention sharpness、位置编码或检索精度，未必能解决高负载下的错误；更值得关注的是层间表示如何保持 item identity、如何减少内容叠加后的判别污染。贡献量级不在于提出新架构，而在于把一个行为现象背后的机制拆清楚，并且给出跨模型族、跨尺度、带干预的证据。","technical_approach":"方法设计很干净。任务上，作者把标准 N-back 以 in-context 形式喂给 15 个预训练 LLM，并控制阈值把“m / -”的对数概率差转成二分类决策。评价上用 d′ 而不是简单 accuracy，避免类别比例和阈值效应把结果掩盖掉。真正关键的是机制判别部分。\n\n第一步是找“执行任务的头”。作者不是平均看所有注意力，而是定位那些对当前决策真正有贡献的 attention heads，然后检查在 lure error 试次上，这些头到底是否仍然把注意力放在 target 位置。如果 precision account 正确，错误试次里应当明显偏离 target；如果 interference account 正确，错误发生时 attention 仍应大体落在 target。\n\n第二步是做可解码性检验。作者从模型内部激活读出：即便模型最终把 lure 当成 match，内部表征里是否仍然保留了“目标位置是否与当前字母相同”和“lure 位置是否相同”这类可分信息。结果支持 interference account：正确决策所需信息并没有消失，问题出在这些信息没有被干净地读到输出上。\n\n第三步是做因果干预。作者比较两类操作：一类是 sharpen attention，让注意力更集中；另一类是擦除或抑制竞争项目的内容表示，直接减少干扰。如果前者收益小、后者收益大，就说明瓶颈不在定位而在内容混叠。论文给出的结论正是后者更有效。\n\n第四步是用从零训练的小 transformer 做边界条件分析。作者发现单层 attention 模型确实更符合 precision account，因为它只能靠一次 softmax 直接完成位置选择与内容汇聚，邻位竞争会直接造成检索模糊；但加到两层后，模型可以先做较粗定位，再在后续层中重组和净化表示，precision 限制不再是主导因素，错误模式转向 interference。这个对照很重要，因为它解释了为什么一些早期小模型分析会得出“softmax 精度瓶颈”的结论，而这结论不能直接外推到现代多层 LLM。","experiments":"实验覆盖 15 个预训练 LLM，来自 3 个模型家族，规模从 70M 到 14B，外加从头训练的最小化 transformer。任务序列长度为 24，从 20 个辅音字母中采样；每个测试序列包含 4 个 lure；每个模型、每个 N 在 200 条 calibration 序列上定阈值，再在 1000 条独立测试序列上评估。论文首先复现了经典现象：随着 N 增大，d′ 下降，错误集中在 N±1 这类邻近 lure 上。\n\n更关键的结果有几类。其一，在承担任务的关键 heads 上，错误试次中的注意力通常仍峰值落在 target，而不是系统性偏到 lure，这直接削弱了 precision account。其二，线性分类器可以从模型激活中读出 target 与 lure 的区分信息，甚至在模型最终答错的试次上仍然成立，说明信息在表示中存在，但没有被正确转成输出。其三，因果干预里，删除竞争项内容带来的性能改善显著大于单纯 sharpen attention，支持“读出瓶颈”而非“检索瓶颈”。其四，尺度分析显示，这个结论在 200 倍参数跨度上都成立；随着模型变大，任务会分散到更多 heads 上，但瓶颈位置没有变。其五，小模型对照显示单层 transformer 的确受 precision 限制，而第二层足以明显缓解这一限制。摘要里没有给出完整数表，全文的价值更多在图表中的模式一致性和干预前后差异，而不是单个 benchmark 数字。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三件事。第一，看引言里对 precision account 和 interference account 的形式化区分，尤其是两者对“错误试次注意力落点”和“干预收益”的相反预测。第二，看结果部分关于关键 attention heads 的分析图，以及错误试次上 target/lure 可解码性的图，这两处基本决定了论文是否成立。第三，看因果干预实验：attention sharpening 与 competitor erasure 的对比，是整篇最有分量的证据。\n\n如果时间只有 20 到 30 分钟，建议读 Introduction、机制预测图、Section 4 和 Section 5 的主结果，再扫一眼 Section 6 关于单层/双层 transformer 的边界条件。若要深挖，重点想两个问题：一，作者如何定义“task-carrying heads”，这个定义会不会把结论预设进去；二，所谓 interference 更接近 value 混合、残差流叠加，还是后续 MLP/readout 的线性不可分。这个问题和现代 LLM 的 long-context failure、needle retrieval 后的 answer formation 有直接关系。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise mechanistic question: when LLMs show the same N-back signature as humans—performance dropping with larger memory load and lure-driven errors concentrated at nearby serial positions—do they fail for the same computational reason, or is the similarity only behavioral? The authors reduce the explanation space to two testable accounts. The precision account says the model fails to localize the item exactly N steps back because softmax attention cannot separate adjacent positions sharply enough. The interference account says the model does attend to the correct position, but the target content is corrupted during readout by competing recent items. The central goal is to distinguish these accounts in pretrained LLMs and controlled small transformers using internal and causal evidence, not just behavioral fits.","importance":"$52","technical_approach":"$53","experiments":"$54","reading_guide":"$55","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会改你对“attention 没找准所以记不住”的直觉；先看 probe 结果和错误样本上的 head 定位，若成立，后续应把记忆瓶颈分析从检索转向读出层。","en":"This can change the default story that memory failures come from bad attention retrieval; read the probe evidence and error-case head analysis first, then reconsider working-memory bottlenecks as readout-limited."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Dongyu Gong"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","working-memory","n-back","attention","readout-bottleneck"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21176","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2610.21176","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2610.22138","title":"Believed but Not Said: Measuring Hidden Belief Collapse in Multi-Agent LLM Discussions","abstract":"LLM systems increasingly rely on groups of agents that discuss, critique, and check one another, assuming that peers will catch individual errors. 轮；每轮每个 agent 发言一次，顺序随机且每轮重洗；每个 agent 只看到最近 H=5 轮历史（截断记忆），以控制上下文长度并让传播动力学更可比。\n2) 对抗者：k∈{0,1,3} 个 attacker agent，每次都坚持同一个“合理但错误”的答案（固定 false claim），只通过发消息影响他人；不读隐状态、不改权重，攻击面严格限定在对话上下文。\n3) 关键测量：对每个良性 true/manipulated/mixed/uncertain；只把 manipulated 计为感染（infection），mixed/uncertain 不算感染，避免把犹豫当作被洗脑。\n5) 论文的关键发现来自“错配结构”：在三攻击者下，私下感染比例远高于公开背书比例；并且出现与经典从众研究相反的模式——更多是“私下变了、公开不表态/不背书”，而不是“公开随大流、私下坚持”。这解释了为什么只看 transcript 会低估风险。","experiments":"实验覆盖 6 个模型 × 3 个事实 QA 数据集（共 18 个 model-dataset cells），每个 cell 在 k=0/1/3 下对比。\n关键结果（论文在引言与结果节反复强调的数字）：\n- 无攻击者时群体很稳：最终只有 0.6% 的良性 agent 以错误答案结束（final infection）。\n- 1 个攻击者即可显著破坏：最终感染率 44.8%。\n- 3 个攻击者更高：最终感染率 60.5%。\n- “隐藏感染”量化：在三攻击者条件下，暴露轮次中私下持有错误答案的比例 51.6%，但公开背书错误主张的比例只有 19.7%。\n- 错配结构：当 agent 私下仍为真时，公开背书错误几乎不发生（仅 2.3% 的 turns）；但当 agent 私下已错时，公开仍常常不背书，58% 的 turns 选择 hedge 或 ignore。\n- “纠错弱”：私下保持正确的 agent，在公开回合里只有 61.9% 会明确反驳，其余多为沉默/模糊/顺势。\n- “知识不等于鲁棒”：两个模型对同一批事实的掌握率都约 85%，但在攻击下放弃事实的比例差异很大（25% vs 80%），说明鲁棒性更多是对话动力学/顺应性而非静态知识量。\n这些结果共同支持结论：多 agent 并不天然更鲁棒，且仅靠公开讨论记录会系统性低估被操纵程度。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 20–30 分钟）：\n- 先看 Introduction 里对“public A feature change observed on one transformed prompt need not recur, and even a recurring change need not affect a downstream computation. We introduce the redistribution sensitivity score (RSS), which identifies large feature responses that recur in a consistent direction, and evaluate the resulting sup","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-15T04:37:53Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2610_21240.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 SAE 解释里一个很实际但常被忽略的问题：当你对语义上等价或近似等价的 prompt 做表面变换时，稀疏特征的激活会重新分配，单看某个 feature 的变化，很难知道它是偶然波动、稳定重分布，还是会真正影响下游读出。作者要解决的核心问题，是如何在一组相关 prompt 变体中识别“可复现、方向一致、能在留出变体上继续成立”的表示重分布，并把这种重分布与下游线性 readout 的变化、以及最终决策失败区分开来。","importance":"它对预训练研究不是直接改进训练 recipe，而是提供了一种更稳的机制分析工具。当前很多 SAE 分析容易停留在个例 feature 叙事：某个 prompt 改写后 feature A 降了、feature B 升了，于是推断模型内部机制发生了什么。问题是这种结论常常不稳，换个同义改写就可能不复现。作者提出的 RSS 试图把“单次观察”提升为“跨变体可重复的支持集”，这对 interpretability、对齐审计、prompt robustness 分析都更有用。对基座研究员来说，价值在于方法论：如果想用 SAE 去研究 tokenizer 变化、数据风格变化、指令模板变化如何影响内部表示，先要有一个能区分偶然扰动和稳定重分布的统计对象。论文还进一步把表示变化和 readout 贡献拆开，提醒人不要把 feature 变化直接等同于行为原因。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 Redistribution Sensitivity Score，简称 RSS。它不是简单找变化最大的 feature，而是同时要求三个条件：变化幅度大、在多个变体上反复出现、并且方向一致。具体做法是：对某个 clean prompt 及其一组 transformed variants，在目标位置计算每个 SAE feature 的激活变化。先用平均绝对变化 h_f 形成候选池，避免正负抵消；再统计每个 feature 在多少个观测上超过阈值 τ，以及这些响应是否大多同向，得到 recurrence r_f 和 consistency c_f。只有满足 r_f≥ρ、c_f≥κ 的 feature 才有资格进入支持集，最后从中取变化幅度最大的前 K 个，形成 responsive support S。MeanRSS 则用来汇总这个支持集的平均响应强度。\n\n这里有两个设计点值得注意。第一，选择和评估分离。支持集和方向是在 selector variants 上固定出来的，然后在 disjoint held-out variants 上直接评估，不重新挑 feature。这避免了“看过测试集再讲故事”。第二，RSS 的定义不使用 readout 权重，因此它先回答“哪些表示变化是稳定的”，再单独分析“这些变化是否影响某个下游线性 readout”。\n\n在 readout 分析部分，作者对固定线性 probe 做精确分解：某个 recurrent feature change 对 logit 的贡献，取决于激活变化大小、对应 readout weight、方向是否对齐，以及 clean prompt 原本离决策阈值还有多远。这样就把三件事拆开了：表示重分布、readout 分数变化、最终 threshold crossing。这个拆分很重要，因为一个 feature 可以稳定变化，但若 readout 权重很小或多个 feature 贡献互相抵消，就未必导致行为失败。\n\n论文还给出有限样本下的方向稳定性保证。比如 n=5、ρ=κ=0.8 时，入选 feature 至少有 4 次同向响应、至多 1 次反向，因此删掉一次观测也不至于翻转方向。这种约束让“稳定重分布”这个对象更像一个可复验的 support，而不是一次性排序结果。","experiments":"实验覆盖 27 个预训练 SAE，来自 8 个语言模型。作者考察的是 prompt transformation 引起的表示重分布是否能在留出变体上复现，以及这种复现能否解释冻结安全 readout 的变化。主要结果有三类。\n\n第一，RSS 选出的支持集在 held-out variants 上仍呈现可重复的 redistribution profile，说明它抓到的不是 selector 集上的偶然 feature 波动。第二，在两个冻结的 safety readout 审计任务上，虽然 RSS 选 support 时完全不看 readout 信息，但这些 support 仍能重建相当大一部分 held-out logit change，说明 recurrent representation change 与下游读出确实存在稳定联系。第三，把 RSS support 与 clean margin 结合后，可以较好地预测 recurrent failure。文中给出的数字是：两个任务上，median support size 分别为 20 和 19 个 feature，得到的 recurrent failure AUROC 分别为 0.935 和 0.958；而使用 full erosion 的 AUROC 为 0.965 和 0.964。这个差距不大，说明很小的 recurrent support 已经保留了大部分审计效用。\n\n从实验解读看，最有意思的不是 AUROC 本身，而是作者证明了“稳定表示变化”和“行为失败”之间并非一一对应：有些 recurrent changes 只改变 readout 分数但不越阈值，有些则需要结合 clean margin 才能解释失败。这让 SAE 分析从‘找 feature’推进到‘找 feature 如何通过 readout 影响决策’。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但要抓住方法边界。先看 Introduction 和 Method，弄清作者为什么要把 redistribution、readout contribution、decision failure 分开。然后重点看 RSS 的定义：候选池、recurrence、directional consistency、support selection、held-out evaluation，这几步是整篇的骨架。接着看实验里两个 safety readout 的 offline audit，尤其是 support size、held-out logit reconstruction、以及结合 clean margin 后的 failure AUROC。\n\n如果只有 20 分钟，读主文的方法定义、Figure 1、以及结果部分关于 held-out reproducibility 和 AUROC 的表格即可。若要深一点，建议看补充材料里 probe scaling、operator-level averaging、以及 full erosion 对照，因为这些决定 RSS 到底是在抓“真实稳定机制”还是某种数据处理产物。阅读时要始终记住：这篇不是在证明 SAE feature 就是因果单元，而是在给 SAE 分析加一个更严格的可复现性筛子。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a practical problem in SAE-based interpretability: when prompts are transformed in ways that preserve the underlying request, sparse feature activity can be redistributed across the dictionary. Looking at one changed feature is often misleading. The change may be idiosyncratic to one variant, may fail to recur on other paraphrases, or may recur without affecting the downstream computation at all. P 张图片及其视觉事实，让求解必须跨文本和图像取证。作者还设置过滤与验证，尽量排除可被参数知识或替代捷径直接解出的样本。训练部分是 joint RL + OPD。RL 侧用 answer reward 加上一些 lightweight behavior penalties，惩罚冗余验证、过早停止等不良行为；OPD 侧不是对全轨迹做硬蒸馏，而是在学生自己 rollout 的不完美轨迹上，根据 这两块。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses two bottlenecks in training long-horizon multimodal search agents. First, genuinely hard training questions are scarce: many existing multimodal QA examples can be solved from parametric memory or short retrieval, so they do not force sustained evidence acquisition. Second, the learning signal is too coarse: with only terminal answer rewards, the agent gets little guidance on when to continue searching, when to stop, and which intermediate actions are redundant or harmful.","importance":"This is closer to post-training for agents than to core pretraining, but it still offers two useful ideas. One is data construction: instead of relying on more manual annotation, the paper turns difficulty creation into a controllable transformation pipeline over existing multimodal questions. That is relevant to synthetic training data, curriculum design, and iterative dataset refinement. The other is the training interface: it combines RL with on-policy distillation, and applies token-level teacher guidance only when imperfect student trajectories still contain learnable signal. This is a practical recipe for sparse-reward settings where process supervision is expensive. Its impact on pretraining research is limited, but it is a meaningful reference for reasoning and agent post-training.","technical_approach":"$5f","experiments":"Evaluation is organized over ten multimodal search benchmarks, with two main questions in mind: whether Refine actually turns easy multimodal QA data into training examples that matter for long-horizon search, and whether joint RL-OPD outperforms RL alone, distillation alone, or simpler combinations. The main takeaway from the paper is that Refine produces harder questions that are more sensitive to deep-search capability, while the joint training setup improves not only final answer accuracy but also search behavior quality, reducing ineffective steps and incorrect stopping. The excerpt provided here does not include the full result tables, so it would be unsafe to restate exact scores. The most important empirical checks are the ablations: whether Expand, Blur, and Visualize contribute distinct difficulty dimensions, and whether reward-gap-weighted OPD is more stable and useful than fixed-weight teacher guidance.","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the Refine pipeline in Section 3, especially BuildLink, Expand, Blur, and Visualize.
